STRONGEST CLAIM AND STATUS

REFUTED mechanism: even when a real rational hard-BCS pair matrix has zero
diagonal, every off-diagonal entry positive, every fixed-half-filling hard
configuration positive, and condition number at most 65/63, there is no
uniform polynomial-bit mixing guarantee for stationary Markov kernels which
replace fewer than all q selected pairs per step. A quantified exact family
below has reversible spectral gap at most 9*2^n*4^(-L), and arbitrary
stationary kernels in the same move class need at least
floor(4^L/(32*2^n)) steps to mix from a specified positive state to TV 1/4.

This strengthens the baseline disconnected permutation-cycle obstruction
to a dense, irreducible hard law. It excludes every stationary local kernel
in the stated move class, not just one unfavorable Metropolis proposal or a
single tiny determinant ratio. The construction itself admits an acquired
two-endpoint approximation when the displayed error bound is small, so it
is explicitly NOT approximation hardness for general parity counting.

UNRESOLVED main gate: no AP-hardness reduction, generic parity FPRAS,
microscopic defect/auxiliary energy theorem, or external novelty claim is
established here. Exact #P hardness and tiny acceptance probabilities have
not been used as substitutes for approximate hardness.

EXACT THEOREM

Let n=2q>=4, L>=n+2, with sites 0,...,n-1. Let P be the directed-cycle
permutation matrix, P[i,j]=1 exactly when j=i+1 modulo n. Define

 C[i,j]=(i+1)^j for i!=j, and C[i,i]=0,
 M=n^(n-1), H=n! (1+M)^n, delta=2^(-L)/H,
 F=P+delta C, a=2^(-L), r=2^n 4^(-L).

For every q-set I, define its complement J=V\I and

 w(I)=det(F[I,J])^2,
 Z=sum_(|I|=q) w(I)=c_q(F), pi(I)=w(I)/Z.

The identity with the legal c_q is direct: disjoint q-sets cover all n
sites, so J is forced to be I's complement. All pair activities are one.
Let E={0,2,...,n-2}, O={1,3,...,n-1} and B=Omega\{E,O}.

Then:
 (i) F has zero diagonal and strictly positive off-diagonal entries.
 (ii) Every w(I)>0. Thus ordinary one-pair Metropolis on q-sets is
      irreducible; the obstruction does not rely on disconnected support.
 (iii) Every singular value of F is in [1-a,1+a], hence
       kappa_2(F)<=(1+a)/(1-a)<=65/63.
 (iv) w(E),w(O) are in [(1-a)^2,(1+a)^2], and
      R=sum_(I in B)w(I)<=r. In particular pi(E),pi(O)>=1/3 and pi(B)<=r.
 (v) If a stationary kernel K on Omega obeys K(E,O)=0, then
      K(E,B)<=2r. If K is also reversible,
      gap(K)<=9r.
 (vi) For every stationary such K and every integer
      0<=t<=floor(1/(32r)), its distribution from E after t steps has
      TV distance greater than 1/4 from pi.

The condition K(E,O)=0 follows whenever each transition replaces at most
b<q selected pair labels: E and O are disjoint q-sets. No restriction on
how other local probabilities are computed is needed for the theorem.
The result also covers a kernel with extra retained registers if its
stationary marginal is pi and one step cannot move the pair label directly
from E to O: apply the same stationary-flow argument to the entire E fiber.
It does not cover auxiliary states with positive stationary mass outside
Omega, coordinated whole-cycle jumps, or a transition whose local word
contains q pair replacements before being counted as one step.

FULL DERIVATION

1. A rational perturbation with no complementary zero minors.

For ordered, distinct positive numbers x_1,...,x_s and strictly increasing
nonnegative integer exponents j_1,...,j_s, det(x_i^(j_l)) is nonzero. If it
were zero, a nonzero real polynomial with at most s distinct monomials
would vanish at the s distinct positive x_i. Such a polynomial has at most
s-1 distinct positive roots: factor out its smallest power, then apply
Rolle's theorem to its derivative, which has at most s-1 monomials, and
induct. The base case of one monomial has no positive root. Therefore every
square generalized Vandermonde minor is nonzero. For disjoint row and
column sets, none of C's deleted diagonal positions occurs. In particular
det(C[I,J]) is a nonzero integer for every disjoint pair I,J of equal size.

For any square s-minor, s<=n, the polynomial
 p_IJ(t)=det(P[I,J]+t C[I,J])
has integer coefficients. Its sum of absolute coefficient values is at
most s!(1+M)^s<=H, by expanding each of the s! determinant permutations
and each product of s entries. For disjoint I,J it is not identically zero,
since its top coefficient is det(C[I,J])!=0.

Write d for its first nonzero degree. That coefficient is a nonzero integer,
so has absolute value at least 1. At t=delta<=1, the remaining tail is
bounded by delta^(d+1) H. Since delta H=a<=1/64<1,

 |p_IJ(delta)| >= delta^d (1-delta H)>0.

This proves positivity of every hard configuration without random-generic
arguments or uncharged testing of exponentially many supports.

2. The original cycle has exactly two full-basis hard configurations.

P[I,J] has a nonzero determinant iff the cycle successor maps I onto J.
For J=V\I this means site membership alternates along the entire n-cycle.
Because n is even, the only possibilities are I=E and I=O. Their minors
are permutation matrices and their determinants have absolute value 1.
Every other I has p_IJ(0)=0. The coefficient-sum bound now implies

 |p_E(delta)-p_E(0)|<=delta H=a,
 |p_O(delta)-p_O(0)|<=delta H=a,
 |p_I(delta)|<=delta H=a for I outside {E,O}.

Consequently endpoint squared weights lie between (1-a)^2 and (1+a)^2,
and R<=binom(n,q)a^2<=2^n a^2=r.

3. Good conditioning does not prevent the bottleneck.

||C||_2<=||C||_F<=nM and H>=nM. Therefore
||F-P||_2<=delta nM<=a. As P is orthogonal, the triangle and reverse
triangle inequalities on ||Fx|| give singular values in [1-a,1+a].
Since n>=4 and L>=n+2, a<=1/64. Thus the condition-number assertion follows.
The graph Slater V=[I,F^T] is likewise full rank and well conditioned; its
nonzero singular values are sqrt(1+sigma_i(F)^2).

Also a<=1/16 and r<=1/16. Hence

 Z>=2(15/16)^2>1,
 Z<=2(17/16)^2+1/16=594/256,
 pi(E),pi(O)>=(225/256)/(594/256)=225/594>1/3,
 pi(B)=R/Z<=r.

4. A stationary-flow bound, rather than acceptance heuristics.

Stationarity gives
 pi(E) K(E,B) <= sum_x pi(x)K(x,B)=pi(B).
Because K(E,O)=0, the only exit from E is into B. Thus

 e=K(E,Omega\{E})<=pi(B)/pi(E)=R/w(E)<=2r.

For reversible K, test its Dirichlet variational characterization with
h=1_E. Then

 E_K(h,h)=pi(E)K(E,B)<=pi(B),
 Var_pi(h)=pi(E)(1-pi(E))>=pi(E)pi(O)>=1/9,
 gap(K)<=E_K(h,h)/Var_pi(h)<=9r.

This is the usual 1-lambda_2 gap; periodicity cannot improve the claimed
worst-case TV mixing guarantee. Laziness is not needed for the upper bound.

For arbitrary stationary K, starting at E, the probability of never leaving
E in t steps is (1-e)^t>=1-te>=1-2tr. Therefore

 TV(K^t(E,.),pi)>=K^t(E,E)-pi(E)
                 >=1-pi(E)-2tr>=pi(O)-2tr>=1/3-2tr.

At t<=1/(32r), this is at least 1/3-1/16=13/48>1/4. This proves (vi)
without reversibility. For an extra-register chain, initialize from its
stationary conditional law on the E fiber; the union bound on the stationary
exit flux gives the same fiber escape estimate. Therefore at least one
actual starting register state has the claimed worst-case slow mixing.

5. Explicit easy counting/sampling boundary for the same family.

Compute the two endpoint determinants exactly and output
 Z0=w(E)+w(O). Then 0<=Z-Z0=R<=r and
 |Z-Z0|/Z=pi(B)<=r. Independently sample E or O with their exact two weights.
The output law is pi conditioned on {E,O}, so its TV distance from pi is
exactly pi(B)<=r. Rational Bernoulli sampling can use integer rejection
with expected O(log(1+numerator denominator)) random bits, or a capped
O(log(1/eta)) extra-bit approximation with charged additive eta failure.
The output law requires only two polynomial-bit determinants and one
rational random-bit decision; no unknown partition, balancing ratio or
inverse rare-event probability is supplied for free.

This admits prescribed accuracy eps whenever the explicit bound r<=eps;
the theorem does not promise arbitrary-accuracy counting for every fixed
member, nor arbitrary conditioned-prefix sampling when both endpoints are
excluded. For fixed n, full enumeration is an alternative exact algorithm,
which makes the absence of any complexity-hardness conclusion particularly
transparent.

COSTS / SUPPLIED-ACQUIRED BOUNDARIES

The family is explicit from n and L. log_2 M=O(n log n),
log_2 H=O(n^2 log n), and each rational entry has
O(L+n^2 log n) bits. Writing the n-by-n matrix costs
O(n^2(L+n^2 log n)) bit output, up to standard encoding overhead. Exact
determinants of rational q-minors are polynomial bit computations. There
is no unbounded precision, unknown support oracle, free coefficient oracle,
free balancing data, or free sampler in the obstruction.

For any fixed move radius b, choose fixed n=2(b+1), then let L grow. The
input length is Theta(L) for that dimension, while the mixing lower bound
is exp(Omega(L)). If n also grows, the precise bound 4^L/(32*2^n) and the
full matrix encoding cost above must be retained rather than calling every
growth regime exponential in the entire encoding length.

positive weights, exactly two unperturbed supports, determinant and bridge
bounds, stationary endpoint masses, condition-number certificates, and the
exact singleton conductance of a uniform single-pair Metropolis chain.
No numerical eigenvalue is needed for acceptance. Decimal values in the
JSON are explicitly diagnostic; integer/Fraction assertions decide checks.

   37a01798e92a0763e64a4649ae00024079d2a266. Its Chen-Liu conditional
   projected-Slater compiler does not cover generic BCS parity. No import
   from that card is needed for the self-contained obstruction proof.

Searches did not provide a verified AP-hardness reduction for this exact
legal class. They were targeted, not exhaustive priority clearance. The

specific scoped family is UNVERIFIED. No field-breaking advance is claimed.

c03_s03 0fda09643eecc98bd1dcc21a0c8cee0fccb2df5147612fb9f30aedd5e4c1ea77
Source: outputs/round6/frozen_initials/c03_s03/INITIAL.txt
c03_s03 INDEPENDENT INITIAL CHECKPOINT
7 October 2026. Requested gpt-6.1-sol / max; observed model UNKNOWN.
Author-derived theorem and complete small reference implementation; independent
verification and exact priority review pending. No peer results were read.

STRONGEST CLAIM

An explicit ordered complex-rational pair matrix F has a deterministically
acquired exact MPS with bond dimensions

 D_p=2^(rank F[[0,p),[p,n)] + rank F[[p,n),[0,p)]).

Consequently, if the maximum displayed rank sum c is O(log n), every canonical
coefficient, every physical occupation prefix norm, exact zero detection, and
independent exact Born sampling of arbitrary nonnegative rational onsite
squared filters are polynomial-bit tasks. Hard projection is included, as are
arbitrary attraction t>2, cancellations, singular F, and rare sectors. The
class is recognized from the supplied order by ordinary exact rational rank,
with no numerical threshold, supplied norm, singular-value promise, or Chen–Liu
FPRAS dependency. Order optimization is not supplied or claimed.

This admits dense nonbipartite full-rank matrices: F_ij=1 for i<j, F_ij=2 for
i>j, F_ii=0 has c=2 and determinant (-1)^(n-1)(2^n-2), n>=2. It is neither
monomial nor cross-bipartite. General quasiseparable matrices, bounded bandwidth
matrices, and low-rank matrices also enter. The unrestricted parity FPRAS is
still open. Gaussian MPS plus local projections is established prior art;
novelty of this exact rational gauge and costed admission theorem is UNKNOWN.

EXACT INPUT AND OUTPUT

Input F in Q(i)^(n x n), in a given site order; t_i>=0 rational; optional pair
count k; optional site restrictions X_i subset {0,u,d,ud}. Let

 C_k = sum_{|I|=|J|=k} |det F[I,J]|^2 product_{i in I intersection J} t_i.

At t_i=0 use 0^0=1. Hard t=0 gives precisely the supplied frontier's disjoint
minor partition. An occupation restriction simply removes forbidden terms.
The delivered code returns all C_k exactly as rationals, rejects zero sectors,
and samples occupations with their exactly normalized squared-minor weights.
It prepares no quantum circuit or hardware state. Acquiring or optimizing the
physical pair matrix lies outside the explicit-input interface.

FULL CONSTRUCTIVE DERIVATION

1. Work in the site-interleaved fermionic mode order
 u_0,d_0,u_1,d_1,..., with exterior multiplication tracking every sign. Write
 G=sum_ij F_ij u_i^dagger d_j^dagger. All quadratic creation monomials commute
and square to zero, so exp G=product_ij(1+F_ij u_i^dagger d_j^dagger).
Its coefficient on I,J equals (-1)^(k(k-1)/2) det F[I,J] in up-then-down order.
Changing to site order adds the explicit inversion parity sum_{i in I,j in J}
1_(i>j). Thus squared coefficients are the required determinant weights.

2. At cut p let L=[0,p), R=[p,n), a=rank F[L,R], b=rank F[R,L]. Factor the
two cross blocks into full rank factors. Their cross generator is a sum of a
products (left-up linear form)*(right-down linear form), and b products
(right-up linear form)*(left-down linear form). Expanding its commuting
nilpotent factors gives 2^(a+b) tensor products. Left factors, before the
invertible local exp G_LL, are all exterior products of subsets of a independent
up forms and b independent down forms. These are linearly independent because
the two spin spaces are disjoint. The right factors have the same property.
Multiplication by exp G_LL and exp G_RR is invertible, with inverse exp(-G).
Therefore the exact unfiltered Schmidt rank is 2^(a+b), and its left Schmidt
support has the following explicit rational basis:

 L_p[S,T] = exp(G_LL) product_{alpha in S} U_alpha
                         product_{beta in T} V_beta.

U is any column basis of F[L,R], viewed as up creation forms. V is any column
basis of F[R,L]^T, viewed as down creation forms. The implementation chooses
actual columns using exact Gaussian elimination, introducing no irrational
normalization. Labels S,T are ordered subsets, with up forms first.

3. Acquire a small, invertible evaluation matrix for this left basis. Select
a physical up rows of U forming a nonsingular a x a minor and b physical down
rows of V forming a nonsingular b x b minor. Let P_p be these c_p=a+b modes.
Evaluate every basis column on every occupation subset Y of P_p, with all
other left modes empty. The resulting square matrix K_p has size D_p=2^c_p.

K_p is invertible: restriction to P_p sends U and V to invertible linear
changes of their respective a- and b-mode spaces. Their induced maps on the
full exterior algebra are invertible. Restriction of exp G_LL acts on this
same algebra by exp(G restricted to P_p), which is invertible. K_p is their
matrix product in occupation coordinates. Overlap between the selected site
indices causes no issue: up and down are distinct modes. The endpoint K_0 is
(1), and at p=n the basis has the single column exp G with vacuum amplitude 1.

4. Consecutive Schmidt supports satisfy
 span L_p subset span L_(p-1) tensor C^4.
Indeed, expand the global pure coefficient tensor first across p and then
across p-1. Its right Schmidt factors at p are independent; applying their
dual linear functionals proves the inclusion for every L_p column. Therefore
there exist unique rational matrices A_p^x, x in {0,u,d,ud}, such that

 L_p[b] = sum_{a,x} L_(p-1)[a] tensor |x> (A_p^x)_[a,b].

Evaluate on each previous pivot occupation Y and new site occupation x. Let
H_p^x[Y,b] be that coefficient of L_p[b]. Then K_(p-1) A_p^x=H_p^x, giving
the acquired formula A_p^x=K_(p-1)^(-1) H_p^x. Every query uses at most
c_(p-1)+2 physical modes, and is computed by exterior multiplication after
erasing all unqueried modes. No full Fock vector is constructed. Induction
from L_0=(1) proves that A_1^x1 ... A_n^xn is the exact BCS amplitude, including
its phase. The code implements precisely this formula.

5. Set w_i(x)=1 for empty/up/down and w_i(ud)=t_i, and set w_i(x)=0 for a
forbidden site occupation. The suffix matrices satisfy

 B_n[0]=(1), B_n[q]=(0) for q!=0,
 B_i[q]=sum_x w_(i+1)(x) A_(i+1)^x B_(i+1)[q-u(x)] (A_(i+1)^x)^*,

where u(x)=1 if the up mode is occupied, otherwise zero. Induction shows
B_i[q] is the Gram matrix of all suffix amplitudes of up charge q. It is PSD,
although its entries can be complex rationals. Therefore C_k=B_0[k]_[0,0].
Global unfiltered amplitudes already have equal up/down number, so tracking
the up count alone gives the exact pair sector, with no scalar tilt or sector
rejection. Local filtering and prefix restriction occur inside this exact
contraction. The final scalar is exactly real and nonnegative; code asserts
this invariant.

6. At a chosen physical prefix, retain its unfiltered virtual amplitude row v
and remaining up count q. The four next-state masses are exactly

 m_x=w_i(x) (v A_i^x) B_i[q-u(x)] (v A_i^x)^*.

Previously accumulated filter weights cancel from the conditional law. These
four nonnegative rational masses sum to the current completion norm. Clear
their denominators and draw an integer uniformly below their total, using
unbiased random bits and rejection of out-of-range integers. Choosing x with
probability m_x/sum m and updating v,q proves the exact Born law by the chain
rule. Each integer attempt has success probability at least 1/2. Exact zero
sector detection precedes any sampling, and positive conditional completions
remain positive. There is no inverse projection or sector probability cost.

BIT AND COMPUTATION COSTS

Let L be the total binary input length and D=max D_p. Deterministic cut ranks,
column bases, and physical pivot rows cost O(n^4) rational field operations by
naive elimination. Their bit cost is polynomial in n,L. Each local exterior
query uses at most c+2 modes and O((c+2)^2 2^(c+2)) arithmetic operations.
There are O(n D^2) such queries. Pivot inversions and tensor multiplications
cost O(n D^3). Thus acquisition has the conservative arithmetic bound
 O(n^4 + n D^3 (c+2)^2).

All coefficient/suffix contractions cost O(n^2 D^3) rational operations and
O(n^2 D^2) rational storage. A reused suffix table gives one sample in
O(n D^2) additional rational operations plus random integer draws. The simple
reference sample() recomputes this table on each call; this remains polynomial
but is not the optimized multiple-sample interface.

For bit heights, clear all F denominators by one Gaussian-rational denominator
of O(L) bits. A queried exterior amplitude has degree at most c+2 in input
entries, with numerator/denominator heights O((c+2)(L+log(c+2))) up to a fixed
constant. A common denominator for K_p has the same polynomial height.
Adjugate/Hadamard bounds make K_p^(-1) and A_p entries have
O(D (c+2)(L+c+2)) bits conservatively. Common denominators for a complete
contraction are products of the per-site tensor denominators and t denominators.
Each term contains only O(n) factors; the number of virtual and physical paths
adds O(n log(4D)) numerator bits. Hence all stored exact rationals have bit
length polynomial in n,D,L. No conditioning/singular-value assumption is
needed for these exact arithmetic bounds.

Uniform c<=C log_2(n+1) for fixed public C makes D<=(n+1)^C and every displayed
time/space/bit bound polynomial in binary input. The implementation has an
explicit max_dimension cap and rejects an exceeded cap; it silently performs
no truncation. Sampling is exact expected polynomial time. Limiting the
integer retries at each site to ceil(log_2(n/delta)) gives an explicit
polynomial worst-time approximate sampler with total failure probability
<=delta; on failure a positive-completion path is acquired by always taking
a positive mass. This truncation wrapper is proved here but not implemented.
Energy, physical gates and backend/token usage are UNKNOWN, not measured.

STRICT EXTENSIONS AND A SECOND EXACT FAMILY

If rank F=r then c<=2r, admitting r=O(log n). If F has bandwidth b, both
cross blocks have rank<=b, admitting b=O(log n). More broadly an r-quasiseparable
matrix has both cross ranks<=r even with full rank and dense nonbipartite
support. This is not an approximation to arbitrary matrices of low entropy:
rank tests are exact and a tiny nonzero singular direction is retained.

For the dense matrix F_ij=alpha if i<j, beta if i>j, and arbitrary diagonal,
hard minors on disjoint I,J vanish unless the colors I,J alternate in their
merged ordered support: consecutive same-color rows or columns are identical
and force a zero minor. For an alternating 2k-site support, row subtraction
gives determinant alpha(alpha-beta)^(k-1) in one orientation and
beta(beta-alpha)^(k-1) in the other, up to the already specified permutation
phase. Consequently, for k>=1,

 C_k=binomial(n,2k)(|alpha|^2+|beta|^2)|alpha-beta|^(2k-2), C_0=1.

For alpha=1,beta=2 this is 5 binomial(n,2k). Row differences
2e_i-e_(i+1) also yield det F=(-1)^(n-1)(2^n-2) at zero diagonal. This fixture
establishes a full-rank, dense, all-size family with c=2 and direct canonical
counting. It does not identify the generic c=2 family with a positive graph
matching representation.

cutrank_bcs.py is a complete Fraction-based complex-rational reference engine,

restrictions, exact zero checks, and an exact expected-time rational sampler.
check_cutrank_bcs.py compares MPS amplitudes with independently evaluated
determinants, and count/prefix sums with direct physical-occupation enumeration.

count vectors, 42 prefix vectors, 48 sampled support checks, diagonal hard
deletion and t=3 attraction, and a 16-site full-rank dense c=2 fixture whose

they do not by themselves prove asymptotics, novelty, or the unrestricted FPRAS.
Initial evidence copies and SHA256 values are preserved in initial_evidence/.

c04_s01 88ebc511329ebc9c80f9bd308053413e210f5276376788e9c7eb5dea3f90b8f0

INITIAL INDEPENDENT CHECKPOINT: O(d)-COVARIANT EB DIRICHLET ROUNDING
Worker c04_s01; requested gpt-6.1-sol/max; observed model UNKNOWN.
7 October 2026. No peer cycle6 result was read or received before this checkpoint.
Status: internally derived complete restricted theorem; unrestricted target OPEN.

EXACT STRONGEST CLAIM
For every d>=2, let Phi:M_d(C)->M_d(C) be UCP, normalized-trace preserving,
positive on the Hilbert-Schmidt space, selfcompatible, and covariant under
conjugation by EVERY real orthogonal d-by-d matrix. Then there is an explicit
positive selfadjoint bistochastic canonical EB channel Psi with

                   I-Psi <= 2(I-Phi).

No supplied instrument-append representation is required. The dimension and
number of tested likelihood directions may grow. Products, mixtures,
unitary conjugates and block-preserving weighted direct sums of this paired
class inherit c=2 by the baseline affine tensor identity. This is a new
restricted extension relative to the supplied baseline, not a proof of
universal rounding or external/priority validation. O(d) covariance, including
its real structure, is an acquired/supplied promise; twirling an arbitrary
channel does not license transfer of the conclusion back to that channel.

1. TWO EIGENVALUES AND NOTATION
Use tau=Tr/d. Decompose any A as
 A_0=tau(A)I,
 A_a=(A-A^T)/2,
 A_s=(A+A^T)/2-tau(A)I.
Orthogonal covariance gives Phi(A)=A_0+lambda_a A_a+lambda_s A_s.
For d>=2 these are the scalar, antisymmetric and symmetric-traceless
O(d) representations. Positivity on HS and the UCP tracial contraction
property imply 0<=lambda_a,lambda_s<=1. These maps are HS selfadjoint.
Let r_a=d(d-1)/2 and r_s=(d-1)(d+2)/2 be their Hermitian real dimensions.
A trace-orthonormal Hermitian basis in each subspace has total normalized
variance V_a=r_a/d and V_s=r_s/d.

2. THREE NECESSARY COMPATIBILITY INEQUALITIES
Let B be any actual two-output compatible broadcaster with common marginal
Phi. Its normalized Choi state omega on R,B,C has input marginal I/d and
satisfies, for trace-orthonormal H in either eigen-subspace,
 Tr omega [H_R^T (H_B+H_C)]=2 lambda/d.
Let P_RB and P_RC be normalized maximally entangled projectors, and let S_RB,
S_RC be the respective swaps (identity on the unused register). Write
 P=P_RB+P_RC, S=S_RB+S_RC. Completeness of the selected Hermitian bases gives

 K_a=sum_(a) H_R^T(H_B+H_C)=(d P-S)/2,
 K_s=sum_(s) H_R^T(H_B+H_C)=(d P+S)/2-(2/d)I,
 K_all=K_a+K_s=d P-(2/d)I.

Their upper spectral bounds are
 K_a <= (d/2)I,
 K_s <= [d/2+3/2-2/d]I,
 K_all <= [d+1-2/d]I.                                      (1)
Consequently selfcompatibility implies

 lambda_a <= d/[2(d-1)],
 lambda_s <= (d+4)/[2(d+2)],
 r_a lambda_a+r_s lambda_s <= (d-1)(d+2)/2.                 (2)

The second inequality is deliberately a non-sharp but sufficient bound.
No numerical SDP or sufficiency claim for (2) enters this proof.

3. COMPLETE DERIVATION OF THE STAR BOUNDS
For v in C^d, use unnormalized contraction vectors
 x1(v)=sum_i |i,i>_RB tensor |v>_C,
 x2(v)=sum_i |i>_R tensor |v>_B tensor |i>_C,
 x3(v)=|v>_R tensor sum_i |i,i>_BC.
Their span W is a direct sum of d copies of a three-dimensional space whose
Gram matrix is G with diagonal d and off-diagonal 1. It is strictly positive
for d>=2. In the ordered x1,x2,x3 basis, the coefficient action matrices are

 dP = [[d,1,1],[1,d,1],[0,0,0]],
 S  = [[1,0,1],[0,1,1],[1,1,0]].                            (3)

These follow directly by contracting/switching indices. W is invariant
under P and S; W-perp is annihilated by P and invariant under S. On W-perp,
||S||<=2, so K_a<=I, K_s<=(1-2/d)I, K_all=-(2/d)I.

On W the K_a matrix is
 [[(d-1)/2,1/2,0],[1/2,(d-1)/2,0],[-1/2,-1/2,0]],
with eigenvalues d/2,(d-2)/2,0, hence its maximum is d/2. The nonorthogonal
basis causes no ambiguity: G>0 and the represented operator is Hermitian.

For K_s, x1-x2 has eigenvalue d/2-2/d. On span{x1+x2,x3} its coefficient
matrix is
 [[(d+2)/2-2/d,1],[1,-2/d]].
The upper eigenvalue is
 [(d+2)+sqrt((d+2)^2+16)]/4-2/d.
It is <= d/2+3/2-2/d because
 (d+4)^2-[(d+2)^2+16]=4(d-1)>=0.
This also exceeds every stated W-perp upper bound. Finally P on W has
eigenvalues 1+1/d,1-1/d,0, proving the K_all bound. Equations (1) and (2)
are therefore analytic all-d statements, not finite extrapolations.

4. TWO EXPLICIT CANONICAL EB ENDPOINTS
For a rank-one projector P_psi, an orbit distribution with E P_psi=I/d
induces the canonical measurement channel
 Psi(A)=d E[P_psi Tr(P_psi A)].
Its POVM effects are d P_psi times the orbit measure and preparations P_psi.
Thus it is EB, unital, trace-preserving, HS selfadjoint, and HS positive.
It is an exact Haar integral with a finite-outcome realization by moment
Caratheodory; physical finite-bit compilation is discussed below.

The real-vector orbit psi=O e1 gives
 Psi_R(A)=[A+A^T+Tr(A)I]/(d+2),
 with eigenvalues mu_a=0, mu_s=2/(d+2).
This follows directly from the real-sphere fourth moment
 E n_i n_j n_k n_l=(delta_ij delta_kl+delta_ik delta_jl+
                    delta_il delta_jk)/[d(d+2)].

The null-vector orbit psi=O(e1+i e2)/sqrt(2), with psi^T psi=0, gives
 Psi_N(A)=[d A-2 A^T+d Tr(A)I]/[(d-1)(d+2)],
 with mu_a=1/(d-1), mu_s=(d-2)/[(d-1)(d+2)].              (4)
A short derivation avoiding an unevaluated Stiefel integral is as follows.
For a pure P=|psi><psi| and t=|psi^T psi|^2,
 ||P_a||_HS^2=(1-t)/2,
 ||P_s||_HS^2=(1+t)/2-1/d.
By O(d) covariance the canonical orbit map is scalar on these irreps.
Its superoperator trace on an irrep is d E||P_irrep||_HS^2, so division
by r_a,r_s gives
 mu_a=(1-t)/(d-1),
 mu_s=[d(1+t)-2]/[(d-1)(d+2)].
Taking t=0 gives (4); t=1 reproduces Psi_R. This simultaneously proves
complete positivity/EB of (4) without an inference from generic PPT.

5. ROUNDING AND ITS FULL CASE CHECK
Set
 m=max(0,2 lambda_a-1), eta=(d-1)m in [0,1],
 Psi=(1-eta)Psi_R+eta Psi_N.
It has eigenvalues
 mu_a=m,
 mu_s=2/(d+2)-[d/(d+2)]m.                                 (5)
These are nonnegative and at most 1. Automatically
 1-mu_a<=2(1-lambda_a).
To prove the symmetric comparison:
 - If lambda_a<=1/2, m=0 and (2)'s separate lambda_s bound gives
   2 lambda_s-1<=2/(d+2)=mu_s.
 - If lambda_a>1/2 and lambda_s<=1/2, then 2 lambda_s-1<=0<=mu_s.
 - If both eigenvalues exceed 1/2, the third inequality in (2) gives
   r_a(2lambda_a-1)+r_s(2lambda_s-1)<=d-1.
   But r_a mu_a+r_s mu_s=d-1 identically in (5), so
   2lambda_s-1<=mu_s.
All cases establish I-Psi<=2(I-Phi) on the entire HS space. Constants act
identically on both maps. This includes arbitrary tested frames, not just
the covariance generators.

6. CONSEQUENCE FOR THE BROADCASTING EXPERIMENT
For ANY likelihood family rho_H=(I+H)/d, H=H*, tau(H)=0, ||H||<=theta,
with r=sup_H ||Phi H-H||_(1,tau), the same Psi obeys
 ||Psi H-H||_(1,tau)^2 <= <H,(I-Psi)H>_tau
 <=2 <H,(I-Phi)H>_tau <=2 theta r.
Therefore one physical EB map reconstructs all family states with
half-trace error <=sqrt(2 theta r)/2. No cardinality, dimension or
spectral-gap factor appears. The original arbitrary broadcasting-to-tracial
reduction retains its imported N46 finite-pair bridge; this theorem alone
needs no such import because it starts with the exact tracial channel.

that the G-weighted compressed operator upper bounds are PSD, using LDL,
for d=2,...,24, and checks comparator formulas for a rational eigenvalue
grid admitted by (2). These are exact transcription checks; equations
(3)--(5) prove the unrestricted d-quantifier within the covariance class.

No optimization solver, bulk corpus ingestion, download or heavy job ran.

8. COSTS / SUPPLIED-ACQUIRED BOUNDARY
The promised real structure/covariance and exact channel description are
inputs, not learned from approximate experiment data. Given its two rational
or computable eigenvalues, (4)--(5) evaluate the map in O(d^2) scalar
arithmetic and polynomial precision; explicit d^4-entry channel matrices
can be formed in their output-size cost. Covariance recognition for a dense
known superoperator is ordinary linear algebra, but this is not an efficient
unknown-apparatus acquisition claim.
The Haar orbit formula certifies EB. A finite exact ensemble exists with at
most O(d^4) atoms by real finite-dimensional moment Caratheodory applied to
P^T tensor P, which also encodes its first moment by partial trace. This
existence assertion does not supply an efficient exact atom search or a
finite-gate compiler. Approximate numerical integration needs its sampling,
precision and approximation costs charged separately; none was executed.
Tensor formulas describe a channel; physical outcome counts multiply across
supplied factors unless a better implementation is acquired. Energy/drive
costs, apparatus learning, token/backend computation costs remain UNKNOWN.

NEXT DECISIVE GATE
For arbitrary positive tracial selfcompatible Phi, derive the all-frame
inequality V-C<=c sum p <H,(I-Phi)H>, with ONE pure reference ensemble in
C, or prove a weaker vanishing collective variance modulus. The present
proof exploits a two-irrep covariance reduction and three scalar witness
bounds. There is no justification for applying it to arbitrary interacting
channels, arbitrary covariance groups, or removing the supplied real
structure. A generic covariance twirl can discard exactly the slow modes
that the requested theorem must retain. Universal c (even c>2), general

c04_s02 6557ecb7bdd0e7449197a40c8529e13e4a0bf6dce1f9a9b211a745469af9660b

WORKER c04_s02: INDEPENDENT COUNTEREXAMPLE ATTACK
7 October 2026. Requested gpt-6.1-sol/max; observed model UNKNOWN.
Initial checkpoint. Candidate internally derived scoped theorem, not externally

STRONGEST CLAIM

No fixed-sandwich counterexample was obtained. An attempted broadening of the
symmetric-copy route instead gives the following all-dimension EB rounding:

Let d>=2, s>=1, N=2s, H_N=Sym^N(C^d), D_N=binomial(N+d-1,N), tau_N=Tr/D_N.
Let T_Ns be physical partial trace from H_N to H_s, and let
 R_sN(X)=(D_s/D_N) P_N(X tensor I)P_N
be its Petz recovery for maximally mixed references. The channel
 Phi_Ns=R_sN T_Ns is positive, trace-selfadjoint, bistochastic and actually
selfcompatible. The coherent-state measure/prepare channel
 Psi_N(A)=D_N integral <z^N|A|z^N> |z^N><z^N| dz
is positive selfadjoint bistochastic EB, and

 I-Psi_N <= c_(s,d)(I-Phi_Ns),
 c_(s,d)=2(s+d)/(2s+d) <2.                                  (1)

The constant is optimal among ALL EB channels satisfying this quadratic
comparison, not just the proposed coherent-state channel. d and s can both
grow arbitrarily. The same universal upper constant 2 survives the already
proved baseline tensor, convex-mixture, conjugation, and weighted direct-sum
closure operations.

Thus on any growing bounded-likelihood experiment rho_H=(I+H)/D_N,
H=H*, tau_N(H)=0, ||H||<=theta, one explicit EB channel obeys

 sup_H T(Psi_N rho_H,rho_H)
   <= (1/2) sqrt(c_(s,d) theta r),
 r=sup_H ||Phi_Ns(rho_H)-rho_H||_1.                         (2)

It is impossible to obtain vanishing actual residual but nonvanishing EB
error inside this corrected bosonic splitting class. This covers arbitrary
bounded H, not only coherent pure-state mixtures or one-particle steering.
It does not represent every compatible channel, nor solve/refute the general
bounded-Holevo broadcasting conjecture.

FULL DERIVATION

A. Actual channel and actual broadcaster.

The canonical grouping V:Sym^(2s)(C^d) -> H_s tensor H_s is an isometry:
a globally symmetric tensor is separately symmetric on its two halves.
The partial traces of V X V* are both T_Ns(X) on every input X. Schur
irreducibility, or the occupation-basis formula below, gives
 T_Ns(I_N/D_N)=I_s/D_s.
The trace adjoint T_Ns* is P_N(X tensor I)P_N and sends I_s to I_N.
Consequently R_sN=(D_s/D_N) T_Ns* is CP, TP, and sends I_s/D_s to I_N/D_N.
The full-domain CPTP map

 B(X)=(R_sN tensor R_sN)(V X V*)                           (3)

has both physical marginals Phi_Ns. It is not a promised-only channel.
On L2(tau_N), Phi_Ns=(D_s/D_N) T_Ns* T_Ns is positive selfadjoint.
Reference preservation makes it bistochastic and a positive contraction.

Explicit finite V in normalized occupation bases, n=k+l:
 V_(k,l),n = sqrt[ product_i binomial(n_i,k_i) / binomial(N,s) ].
Its isometry identity is the multivariate Vandermonde identity. This also
specifies the acquired finite matrices without an oracle.

B. EB channel and the simultaneous spectral decomposition.

Normalized Haar measure dz on pure z in C^d satisfies
 integral |z^N><z^N| dz = I_N/D_N.
Hence the displayed Psi_N is measure/prepare, UCP, TP and reference preserving.
The quadratic form tau_N(A* Psi_N A)=integral |<z^N|A|z^N>|^2 dz is nonnegative;
Psi_N is selfadjoint and 0<=Psi_N<=I by scalar Schwarz.

For clarity the representation step used below is stated precisely. The
coherent symbol map A -> <z^N|A|z^N> identifies End(H_N) with bihomogeneous
polynomials of bidegree (N,N) restricted to the unit sphere. Occupation
monomials give injectivity: a vanishing symbol has every coefficient zero.
Fischer decomposition by the complex Laplacian splits this space into the
harmonic bidegrees (ell,ell), 0<=ell<=N. These are the multiplicity-one U(d)
modules with highest weight (ell,0,...,0,-ell); each is generated by
(bar z_1 z_d)^ell and has dimension
 m_(d,ell)=binomial(ell+d-1,d-1)^2-binomial(ell+d-2,d-1)^2,
where the second term is zero for ell=0. The dimensions telescope to D_N^2.
The highest-weight generator statement also follows directly by solving the
raising-operator differential equations on harmonic polynomials; lowering
operators generate its orbit module. This is the usual polynomial harmonic
representation, not a numerical diagonalization premise. Covariant maps
therefore act by scalars on these orthogonal modules.

Put (n)_ell=n(n-1)...(n-ell+1), and for ell<=N choose
 O_(N,ell)=(a_1^dagger)^ell (a_d)^ell on H_N.
Its coherent symbol is (N)_ell (bar z_1 z_d)^ell. It spans the highest-weight
line (the choice of raising convention only exchanges first/last coordinates).
The Haar moment is elementary Dirichlet integration:

 integral product_i |z_i|^(2r_i) dz
   = (d-1)! product_i r_i! / (sum_i r_i+d-1)!.

Evaluate the Psi_N O matrix element between occupations
 (ell,0,...,0,N-ell) and (0,...,0,N). The prefactors are
 sqrt[binomial(N,ell)] and sqrt[ell!(N)_ell]. Dividing gives

 mu_(N,ell)
   = (N)_ell (N+d-1)!/(N+d+ell-1)!
   = product_(j=0)^(ell-1) (N-j)/(N+d+j).                  (4)

Thus Psi_N acts as mu_(N,ell) on the complete ell module, including mu_(N,0)=1.
No large-N asymptotic is used.

For the partial trace, coherent vectors give exactly

 T_Ns* O_(s,ell) = [(s)_ell/(N)_ell] O_(N,ell), ell<=s.     (5)

Indeed both sides have the same coherent symbol, and the coherent symbol
map is injective. For ell>s the smaller operator space has no such module.
Let Q_Ns=(D_s/D_N)T_Ns map likelihood operators from H_N to H_s. Its adjoint
for the two normalized trace inner products is exactly T_Ns*. Since

 integral |<z^N|O_(N,ell)|z^N>|^2 dz
   = mu_(N,ell) tau_N(O_(N,ell)* O_(N,ell)),

the norm equals (N)_ell^2 times a common ell,d Haar moment divided by mu_N.
Combining this norm formula with (5) yields the singular square of Q_Ns:

 lambda_(N,s,ell) = mu_(s,ell)/mu_(N,ell), ell<=s,
 lambda_(N,s,ell) = 0, ell>s.                              (6)

These are precisely the eigenvalues of Phi_Ns=T_Ns* Q_Ns.

C. Exact scalar deficit comparison.

For N=2s and j=0,...,s-1 set
 a_j=(2s-j)/(2s+d+j),
 l_j=[(s-j)/(s+d+j)]/a_j.
All lie in [0,1], and on ell<=s,
 mu_(N,ell)=product_(j<ell) a_j,
 lambda_(N,s,ell)=product_(j<ell) l_j.
Direct rational subtraction gives

 (1-a_j)/(1-l_j)
   =(2s-j)(s+d+j)/[s(2s+d+j)] <= c_(s,d),                 (7)

because the difference from its j=0 value c_(s,d) is

 j[d^2+d j+3d s+2j s] / [s(d+2s)(d+j+2s)] >=0.

To pass from factors to products WITHOUT assuming a_j<=l_j, let
 A=1-1/c, B=1/c. The factor inequality gives l_j<=A+B a_j.
For u,v in [0,1],

 A+Buv-(A+Bu)(A+Bv)=AB(1-u)(1-v)>=0.

Induction yields product l_j <= A+B product a_j, exactly
 1-mu_(N,ell)<=c(1-lambda_(N,s,ell)).
For ell>s, lambda=0 and 1-mu<=1<c. ell=0 has zero deficits.
The complete orthogonal decomposition proves (1), with no missing directions.

D. Optimality among EB maps.

Take traceless Hermitian t_a on C^d with Tr(t_a t_b)=delta_ab,
a=1,...,d^2-1, and J_a=sum_(i=1)^N t_a^(i) restricted to H_N.
The matrix completeness identities are
 sum_a t_a tensor t_a = Swap-I/d,
 sum_a t_a^2=(d^2-1)I/d.
Every swap is identity on H_N, hence

 sum_a J_a^2 = N(d-1)(N+d) I_N/d,
 V=sum_a tau_N(J_a^2)=N(d-1)(N+d)/d.                       (8)

For every state eta on H_N with one-particle marginal eta_1,
 sum_a [Tr(eta J_a)]^2=N^2[Tr(eta_1^2)-1/d]
   <=N^2(d-1)/d.                                         (9)

Write ANY physical EB endomorphism E as sum_y Tr(M_y X) sigma_y.
Put p_y=Tr(M_y)/D_N, eta_y=M_y/Tr(M_y). Then

 sum_a tau_N(J_a E(J_a))
   =sum_y p_y sum_a Tr(eta_y J_a)Tr(sigma_y J_a)
   <=N^2(d-1)/d = [N/(N+d)] V,                            (10)

by (9) and scalar Cauchy--Schwarz; sum p_y=1. E need not be unital,
selfadjoint or canonical for this bound. All J_a belong to ell=1 and
Phi_Ns(J_a)=lambda_(N,s,1)J_a. Testing I-E<=c(I-Phi_Ns) on this whole frame
therefore forces

 c >= (1-N/(N+d))/(1-lambda_(N,s,1))
   =2(s+d)/(2s+d).                                        (11)

Psi_N attains it. This proves the stated optimality. The tests are Hermitian
quadratic forms; their sum suffices even if an arbitrary E is nonselfadjoint.

E. Operational consequence.

Since 0<=Psi_N<=I,
 ||(I-Psi_N)H||_(1,tau)^2 <=||(I-Psi_N)H||_(2,tau)^2
 <=<H,(I-Psi_N)H> <=c<H,(I-Phi_Ns)H>
 <=c theta ||(I-Phi_Ns)H||_(1,tau).
Dividing physical full trace by two proves (2). The SAME map handles every
label and family size. Capacity on theta-bounded likelihoods is <=ln(1+theta)
by rho<= (1+theta)I/D_N. This result does not rely on pairwise classicality.

C04_S03 INITIAL — GENERAL EB-BRANCH ROUNDING AND COSTED POVM ACQUISITION
Independent checkpoint, 7 October 2026. Origin requested gpt-6.1-sol/max;
observed backend model/effort UNKNOWN. No cycle6 peer outputs read.

STATUS AND STRONGEST CLAIM
The unrestricted positive selfadjoint selfcompatible channel problem is OPEN.
This checkpoint proves an admitted extension of the factor-two construction:
canonical preparations are unnecessary, and weighted-block-changing physical
instruments are allowed when their total channel and EB branch preserve the
central reference. The actual EB reconstruction is explicitly acquired from
the effects of the supplied instrument. Its full-trace residual bound is
  ||Psi(H)-H||_1,tau <= sqrt(2 theta ||Phi(H)-H||_1,tau).
The constant has no dimension, block-weight, outcome-count or label-count
factor. A separate rational POVM compiler has infinity-to-L1 error <=18 e,
independent of the smallest outcome probability. The algebraic proof is
complete below. A small exact Fraction implementation checks a noncanonical,
block-changing fixture. These are internal results; priority, external proof
validation and the unrestricted acquisition gate are not established.

READ BASELINE / DELTA
Read pinned Astra 37a01798e92a0763e64a4649ae00024079d2a266 entry points,
N46/N61 cards, NEW_FRONTIER, ACTIVE_CONTEXT and cycle4 detailed_balance reports.
The baseline used inner bistochastic Kraus K_y, M_y=K_y* K_y and the specific
preparation sigma M_y/tau(M_y). It explicitly excluded arbitrary block-changing
instruments. Here one may use arbitrary reference-preserving physical
instruments and arbitrary preparation ensembles with reference mean sigma.
The key replacement is Q^dagger Q<=Psi_M, not Q=Psi_M. This does not assert
strict set containment against every other baseline closure/representation.

1. SETUP AND MEASUREMENT / PREPARATION CONTRACTIONS
Let A be a finite-dimensional C*-algebra with faithful normalized trace tau,
physically represented by a central faithful density sigma: tau(X)=Tr(sigma X).
All matrix-block dimensions and positive central weights may grow. Let M_y in A
be a finite POVM, sum M_y=I, p_y=tau(M_y)>0; zero effects can be removed by
faithfulness. Supply density states rho_y in A such that
  sum_y p_y rho_y=sigma.
Put A_y=sigma^(-1/2) rho_y sigma^(-1/2), so A_y>=0, tau(A_y)=1 and
sum p_y A_y=I. Centrality allows the equivalent sigma^(-1)rho_y expression.

Use the commutative algebra C^m with weighted trace tau_p(f)=sum p_y f_y.
Define unital CP trace-preserving maps
  T(X)_y=tau(M_y X)/p_y,
  P(f)=sum_y p_y f_y A_y.
T is positive because each coordinate is a state. P is CP because its domain
is commutative and the A_y are positive. Unitality and trace preservation are
exactly the displayed normalizations. Kadison Schwarz followed by the trace
shows ||TX||_2,p<=||X||_2,tau and ||Pf||_2,tau<=||f||_2,p.
The trace adjoint is
  T^dagger(f)=sum_y f_y M_y.
Thus the canonical map
  Psi=T^dagger T,
  Psi(X)=sum_y tau(M_y X) M_y/p_y
is UCP, tau-preserving, selfadjoint and positive as an L2 operator. It obeys
0<=Psi<=I. Its physical Schrödinger implementation is the EB channel
  rho -> sum_y Tr(M_y rho) sigma M_y/p_y.
Central sigma makes every preparation positive; all have trace one.

Let Q=P T. Physically it is the ordinary EB map
  Q_phys(rho)=sum_y Tr(M_y rho) rho_y.
It fixes sigma. The previous contraction proves the load-bearing inequality
  Q^dagger Q=T^dagger P^dagger P T<=T^dagger T=Psi.       (1)
Q need not be positive/selfadjoint as an L2 operator; its preparations may
be pure, rotate, change blocks, or differ from canonical preparations.

2. THE GENERAL FACTOR-TWO IDENTITY
Let L:A->A be ANY UCP tau-preserving channel, hence L^dagger L<=I. Set
  C=(L+Q)/2, Phi=C^dagger C.
Phi is UCP, tau-preserving and positive selfadjoint on L2. Exact expansion:
  2(I-Phi)-(I-Psi)
    =(I-L^dagger L)+(Psi-Q^dagger Q)
                           +(L-Q)^dagger(L-Q)/2 >=0.    (2)
The first term is PSD by Schwarz, the second by (1), the third is a square.
Consequently I-Psi<=2(I-Phi). No local commutation, Q selfadjointness,
Psi<=Phi ordering, spectral gap, minimum p_y or rank hypothesis is needed.
The quadratic comparison itself does not require compatibility of L and Q.

For Hermitian H with ||H||_infinity<=theta, 0<=Psi<=I gives
  ||(I-Psi)H||_1,tau^2
   <=||(I-Psi)H||_2,tau^2
   <=<H,(I-Psi)H>_tau
   <=2<H,(I-Phi)H>_tau
   <=2 theta ||(I-Phi)H||_1,tau.                        (3)
The first step is normalized Holder; the second is (I-Psi)^2<=I-Psi;
the last is trace duality. Physical likelihood states sigma(I+H), tau(H)=0,
||H||<=theta<1 therefore admit ONE EB reconstruction with half-trace error
  e_EB <= (1/2) sqrt(2 theta r),
  r=sup_H ||Phi(H)-H||_1,tau.
The same map handles arbitrarily many supplied or unspecified directions.

3. AN ACTUAL SELFCOMPATIBLE REALIZATION WITH BLOCK TRANSITIONS
Supply physical CP instrument maps I_y on A with effects M_y=I_y^*(I),
sum_y I_y=L_phys CPTP, and L_phys(sigma)=sigma. Effects and states are in A;
the maps may move matrix blocks. Define the likelihood coordinate channel
  L(X)=sigma^(-1/2) L_phys(sigma^(1/2)X sigma^(1/2)) sigma^(-1/2).
It is UCP because L_phys fixes sigma, and tau-preserving because L_phys is TP.
Unlike the baseline inner-Kraus formula, this argument allows block movement.

The full-domain CP instrument-append channel is
  B_0(rho)=sum_y I_y(rho) tensor rho_y.
It is TP, and its marginals are L_phys and Q_phys. Average B_0 with its output
swap; both marginals become C_phys=(L_phys+Q_phys)/2, which fixes sigma.
The actual Petz recovery of C_phys at sigma is
  R_phys(z)=sigma^(1/2) C_phys^*(sigma^(-1/2)z sigma^(-1/2)) sigma^(1/2).
This is CP and TP; trace preservation follows from C_phys(sigma)=sigma.
In central likelihood coordinates it is C^dagger. Applying R_phys to BOTH
outputs of the actual symmetrized joint channel gives common marginal
Phi_phys(rho)=sigma^(1/2) Phi(sigma^(-1/2)rho sigma^(-1/2)) sigma^(1/2).
Thus Phi really is selfcompatible, not merely an algebraic positive map.
For represented direct sums, first pinch input block coherences; all promised
states are fixed. Embed output blocks physically. This yields ordinary CPTP
channels and an ordinary EB reconstruction on the full ambient matrix algebra.
Rho_y need not share a block with M_y. Only the reference-mean condition is
required. Arbitrary instruments without L_phys(sigma)=sigma do not qualify.

4. EXACT EXECUTED NONCANONICAL, BLOCK-CHANGING FIXTURE
A=M2 direct-sum M2, reference block weights (1/3,2/3), sigma=diag(1/6,1/6,1/3,1/3).
Physical block transition probabilities P[output][input] are
  [[1/2,1/4],[1/2,3/4]], with stationary weights as stated.
Within each input block use Kraus K_0=diag(4/5,3/5), K_1=diag(3/5,4/5).
For each input a, output b and outcome y the physical CP branch is
P[b][a] times K_y rho_a K_y* embedded in block b. Its effect is P[b][a]
times K_y*K_y in block a. Prepare rho_(a,b,y)=pure |y><y| in output block b.
These preparations differ from sigma M_y/p_y and can move blocks.
Their p-weighted mean is exactly sigma. Sum effects is exactly I.

In a block Pauli basis the likelihood block matrix is
  R=[[1/2,1/2],[1/4,3/4]],
L acts by R times (1,24/25,24/25,1), Q by R times (1,0,0,7/25), and
Psi acts blockwise by (1,0,0,49/625). Phi=C^dagger C uses weighted Gram
diagonal (1/3 four times,2/3 four times).
weighted_instrument_check.py uses standard-library Fraction, no optimizer.
It exactly verifies the generalized SOS, weighted selfadjointness, effect and
reference normalization; it checks all 255 principal minors for each of the
three 8x8 PSD matrices I-L^dagger L, Psi-Q^dagger Q and the factor-two slack.
It also checks the dimension-free squared full-trace error on 125 rational
diagonal likelihood fixtures with theta=1/2. Runtime observed ~0.041 seconds.
Artifact: work/cycle6/c04_s03/weighted_instrument_check.json.
The all-dimension theorem follows from (1)-(3); fixtures verify transcription.

5. RARE-OUTCOME-SAFE CANONICAL POVM CONTINUITY
For any positive effects a,b and Hermitian ||H||<=1 define
  F_H(a)=tau(a H) a/tau(a), F_H(0)=0.
Write p=tau(a), q=tau(b); assume p>=q, interchanging if necessary. Then
  F_H(a)-F_H(b)=[tau(aH)/p](a-b)
                    +b[tau(aH)/p-tau(bH)/q].
The first term has L1 norm <=||a-b||_1. The second norm is
  |(q/p)tau(aH)-tau(bH)|
   <=(q/p)|tau((a-b)H)|+((p-q)/p)|tau(bH)|
   <=||a-b||_1+|p-q| <=2||a-b||_1.
The q=0 case follows directly. Hence
  ||F_H(a)-F_H(b)||_1<=3||a-b||_1,
  ||Psi_M-Psi_N||_(infinity->1,tau)
      <=3 sum_y ||M_y-N_y||_1,tau.                     (4)
Matched zero effects are allowed. There is no division by p_min in the bound.
The maps each remain canonical EB and preserve the exact reference.

6. FINITE-BIT POVM COMPILER WITH EXACT POSITIVITY / NORMALIZATION
For a supplied algebraic POVM with m effects, choose rational e>0 and round
Hermitian entries to rational grid spacing q<=e/(2 m d), where d is the total
ambient matrix size. Enforce the same conjugate entries; preserve block zeros.
The operator error of each rounded matrix B_y is <=e/m=:u (row-sum bound).
Set N_y=B_y+u I. Then 0<=N_y-M_y<=2u I, and
  I<=S=sum N_y<=I+2e I.
Define exact rational effects
  M'_y=N_y/(1+2e), M'_0=I-S/(1+2e).
They are PSD and sum to I; a zero remainder can be dropped. Also
  sum_y ||M'_y-M_y||_1 + ||M'_0||_1 <=6e.
For original outcomes, triangle inequality gives <=4e in total; the remainder
has trace <=2e. Equation (4), with original zero remainder, proves
  ||Psi_M'-Psi_M||_(infinity->1,tau)<=18e.               (5)
The rounded map's full-trace experiment error is therefore at most
  sqrt(2 theta r)+18 theta e                            (6)
for the admitted class above, and its half-trace error is half this quantity.
This gives a concrete precision budget e=eta/(18 theta) for additive full
error eta. The coefficient 18 is conservative, not asserted sharp.

Storage: O(m d^2) rational entries with O(log(m d/e)) new rounding bits per
real/imaginary entry, plus supplied reference bits and its block representation.
One common rational grid makes nonzero outcome traces rational of controlled
bit length; exact normalized preparations sigma M'_y/tau(M'_y) have polynomial
bit length, even for rare outcomes. Algebraic input entry evaluation to this
grid may have large root-isolation cost; that is charged to the supplied
algebraic representation, not hidden by the dimension-free analytic bound.
No constant apparatus size, physical gate cost or dimension-independent CPU
runtime follows. General PSD-state / POVM quantum compilation remains an
additional dimension-dependent task; (5) is a classical finite specification.

7. ACQUISITION FROM A BARE RATIONAL CHANNEL: PRECISE DECISION ROUTE
A supplied compatible channel or joint Choi does NOT supply the instrument /
EB-branch decomposition above. Nor does it supply a universal c=2 theorem.
An exact finite-dimensional search can nevertheless separate this scientific
question from existence-to-construction debt.
For A=M_d, use rank-one POVM effects d w_y |v_y><v_y|, with ||v_y||=1,
w_y>=0 and sum w_y |v_y><v_y|=I/d. The canonical channel is
  Psi(X)=d sum_y w_y <v_y|X|v_y> |v_y><v_y|.
Rank-one refinement only increases Psi as a Hilbert quadratic form. Preserve
the mean and the real symmetric second moment of the traceless Hermitian
coordinates of |v><v|. If n=d^2-1, Caratheodory gives at most
  m=1+n+n(n+1)/2=d^2(d^2+1)/2
outcomes preserving both moments, hence the same canonical channel.
Therefore feasibility of I-Psi<=c(I-Phi), or its additive delta-I relaxation,
reduces to a finite real semialgebraic sentence. Use rational Hermitian matrix
coordinates (unnormalized Pauli-like symmetric/imaginary matrix units), impose
unit norms, nonnegative weights, reference mean and PSD of the quadratic
slack. All coefficients are rational for rational input Phi,c,delta; denominators
can be cleared positively. Exact real quantifier elimination can return
algebraic witnesses, or an exact infeasibility certificate. The compiler above
then produces rational EB specifications with a rigorous bounded-experiment
error. No PPT==separability substitution is used.

This is a specified complete finite decision reduction, NOT an executed QE
engine or efficient algorithm. Variables are O(d^5), polynomial degree is
bounded at fixed encoding, and generic real-algebraic search can be enormous.
I do not assign an unsupported practical exponent. Dense marginal Choi input
already has O(d^4) entries; dense joint Choi on d x d x d has O(d^6) entries.
Reading the latter costs Omega(d^6 B) bit operations for B-bit explicit entries.
At m=O(d^4), the acquired POVM list has O(d^6) entries. Dimension-uniform error
constants do not mean dimension-uniform representation costs. A channel
oracle, physical apparatus, or sparse circuit does not silently supply an
exact dense rational Choi matrix; their acquisition costs remain separate.

CP POWER-RANGE LIFTING: TWO DIMENSION-FREE RESTRICTED CONSTRUCTIONS
Worker c05_s01, requested gpt-6.1-sol/max, observed model UNKNOWN.

STATUS AND STRONGEST CLAIM

The universal gate beta_m -> 0 is UNRESOLVED. No field-breaking result,
constant-error compatible counterexample, or universal EB lift is claimed.
Two restricted statements below have complete independent derivations and
bounded executable checks. They await independent verification and priority
review. Their supplied anchor/broadcaster inputs are substantive premises.
The second does NOT need Hilbert-space positivity or selfadjointness.

(A) A UCP trace-preserving map T with uniformly almost commuting ENTIRE
Hermitian contraction range has an explicit dimension-free EB lift whenever
a supplied complete diagonal sign frame has bounded approximate preimages.
With kappa the preimage norm bound, a the RMS L2 anchor error and delta the
uniform range commutator bound,

 inf_EB ||T-EB||_(infinity -> 1, Hermitian)
     <= (kappa delta + 2a)/sqrt(2).                         [A]

The constructed map is E_D T. There are fewer than 2r sign anchors for r
minimal projections of a supplied MASA, regardless of the physical dimension
or weighted trace spectrum. For positive selfadjoint T, E_D T E_D is also a
positive selfadjoint EB map, with twice the displayed norm bound.

(B) Let Phi be UCP and trace preserving, with a supplied actual two-output
selfcompatibilizing channel. Let D be a supplied maximal diagonal algebra
with rank-one projections P_i. Its average classical leakage is

 eta = 1 - sum_i tau(P_i Phi(P_i)).

There is an explicit reference-preserving EB channel E with

 ||Phi-E||_(infinity -> 1, Hermitian) <= 2 sqrt(eta) + eta.   [B]

In fact the normalized reference-Choi states have that trace-norm bound.
The construction uses a classical matching projection of the actual
broadcaster Choi state followed by a positive deficit completion. It keeps
one common EB map for every bounded input and has no label-count factor.
It is valid on weighted central tracial algebras. It does NOT give a uniform
diamond bound: normalized Choi trace distance is not diamond distance.

READ INTERFACE AND BASELINE

Read: cycle6 COMMON and own assignment, latest pinned Astra 00_START_HERE /
01_CORE, research_state NEW_FRONTIER and ACTIVE_CONTEXT, broadcasting parts
of the inventory, cycle3 sandwich_broadcast_algebra Section 10, and cycle4
detailed_balance_broadcast_attack Sections 1 and 5. Selectively retrieved
N46 at commit 37a01798e92a0763e64a4649ae00024079d2a266. No peer result was read.

Baseline map interface: a finite-dimensional C*-algebra A, faithful
normalized trace tau, represented physically by a central faithful density
sigma; positive tau-selfadjoint selfcompatible UCP Phi. The baseline gives

 delta_m <= 2/sqrt(m+1)+4/(m+1)

for the ENTIRE Hermitian contraction range of Phi^m. The desired quantity is
sup over all dimensions/traces/Phi of inf_EB ||Phi^m-EB||_(infinity -> 1).
The N46 finite-pair bridge, Petz transport, centralization and bounded-Holevo
reduction remain imported/scoped baseline inputs. Neither new lemma removes
those dependencies for the original experiment conjecture.

1. A COMPLETE SIGN FRAME AND ITS EXACT PINCHING IDENTITY

Let P_1,...,P_r be mutually orthogonal minimal projections inside each matrix
block, summing to I; D=span(P_i) is a MASA in A. Write p_i=tau(P_i)>0.
The trace-preserving conditional expectation is

 E_D(X)=sum_i tau(P_i X) P_i/p_i.

Choose k=ceil(log_2 r), assign distinct v_i in F_2^k, and for t in F_2^k set

 U_t=sum_i (-1)^(v_i dot t) P_i,       N=2^k<2r (r>1).

For r=1 use N=1. These are Hermitian unitaries and character orthogonality
makes

 (1/N) sum_t U_t X U_t = E_D(X).

For any X in A, traciality then gives the EXACT identity

 (1/N) sum_t ||[X,U_t]||_2^2 = 2||X-E_D(X)||_2^2.           [1]

It follows either by expanding the two commutator products and averaging,
or by matrix entries: the average of (s_i-s_j)^2 is 2 off the diagonal and
0 on the diagonal. Central block weights cancel in precisely the same way.
This uses r growing anchors but does not multiply the approximation error
by r. No random sampling is needed to implement the finite frame.

2. PROOF OF THE APPROXIMATE PREIMAGE LIFT [A]

Assume the following inputs are SUPPLIED, not acquired by commutator decay:
Hermitian B_t in A with ||B_t|| <= kappa, satisfying

 a^2 = (1/N) sum_t ||T(B_t)-U_t||_2^2.

Let delta bound ||[T(A),T(C)]||_2 for every pair of Hermitian contractions
A,C. For an arbitrary Hermitian contraction A, put X=T(A); ||X||<=1.
By homogeneity of the entire-range assumption,

 ||[X,U_t]||_2
   <= ||[T(A),T(B_t)]||_2 + ||[X,U_t-T(B_t)]||_2
   <= kappa delta + 2||U_t-T(B_t)||_2.

Minkowski in the finite t-average, followed by [1], yields

 ||T(A)-E_D T(A)||_2 <= (kappa delta+2a)/sqrt(2).

Normalized trace Holder gives the same upper bound in L1. This is uniform
in A. E_D T is UCP, tau preserving, unital and EB, with the explicit form

 E_D T(A)=sum_i tau(T^*(P_i) A) P_i/p_i.

The effects T^*(P_i) are positive and sum to I; the preparations P_i/p_i
have tau=1. Thus no approximate CP repair is hidden in [A]. The physical
map is ordinary measure-and-prepare after the central-reference conversion.

If T is tau-selfadjoint and positive as an L2 operator, put F=E_D T E_D.
It is UCP, tau preserving, selfadjoint, Hilbert-positive and EB. Write
T-F=(I-E_D)T+E_D T(I-E_D). Duality of the Hermitian infinity-to-one norm and
selfadjointness give

 ||E_D T(I-E_D)||_(infinity->1)
   =||(I-E_D)T E_D||_(infinity->1)
   <=||(I-E_D)T||_(infinity->1),

because E_D is an operator-norm contraction. Therefore its error is at most
twice [A]. No Dirichlet domination is claimed by this norm estimate.

Power-range consequence: if for each Phi^m a supplied D and anchors obey
kappa_m delta_m + a_m ->0 uniformly over the admitted sequence, then beta_m
for that sequence tends to zero. This conditional statement retains every
uniform quantifier. The baseline gives delta_m, NOT kappa_m or a_m.

3. PROOF OF THE BROADCASTER PROJECTION-AND-DEFICIT CONSTRUCTION [B]

Use the physical representation of A with central sigma. Choose a basis
compatible with its blocks and D, so sigma=sum_i p_i P_i and p_i=tau(P_i).
Let B_phys be an actual CPTP map to two receiver systems with both marginals
Phi_phys and Phi_phys(sigma)=sigma. Use the normalized reference purification

 |Omega_sigma> = sum_i sqrt(p_i)|i>_R |i>_input,
 Omega_RBC=(id_R tensor B_phys)(|Omega_sigma><Omega_sigma|).

Both receiver marginals and the R marginal are sigma. Define the orthogonal
matching projection

 P = sum_i P_i^R tensor I_B tensor P_i^C.

Its success probability is

 Tr(P Omega)=sum_i p_i Tr[P_i Phi_phys(P_i)]
             =sum_i tau(P_i Phi(P_i))=1-eta.

For each i define a positive operator on B by a partial matrix element,

 u_i = <i_R,i_C|Omega|i_R,i_C>/p_i.

Its trace is at most 1; put a_i=1-Tr(u_i)>=0. Tracing receiver C in the
successful projected state eliminates every R off-diagonal block:

 Tr_C(P Omega P)=sum_i p_i P_i^R tensor u_i.                [2]

Let

 delta_B=sigma-sum_i p_i u_i.

This is positive. To see it without subtracting unrelated positive
operators, put Q=I-P. Partial trace over R,C kills P Omega Q and Q Omega P
because P is diagonal in the traced R,C indices. Hence

 delta_B=Tr_RC(Q Omega Q)>=0,       Tr delta_B=eta.

If eta>0 set R_i=u_i+a_i delta_B/eta. Then R_i>=0, Tr R_i=1, and

 sum_i p_i R_i=sum_i p_i u_i + delta_B=sigma.

Thus the full-domain channel

 E_phys(rho)=sum_i Tr(P_i rho) R_i

is EB, CPTP and fixes sigma EXACTLY. All R_i belong to A, hence commute with
central sigma; in likelihood coordinates it is an EB_tau UCP map with
preparations sigma^(-1)R_i. If eta=0 positivity forces Omega=P Omega P,
a_i=0 and R_i=u_i, so the same formula gives E=Phi exactly.

Its normalized reference-Choi state is

 Omega'_RB=sum_i p_i P_i^R tensor R_i
           =Tr_C(P Omega P)+(sum_i p_i a_i P_i^R) tensor delta_B/eta.

The final added positive term has trace eta. For completeness, the
projection gentle bound used here needs no imported theorem. A pure vector
with projected success 1-x has

 || |psi><psi|-P|psi><psi|P ||_1 = sqrt(x(4-3x)) <=2sqrt(x),

by diagonalizing its 2 by 2 success/failure block. Apply this to a pure-state
decomposition of Omega and use concavity of sqrt to obtain

 ||Omega-P Omega P||_1 <=2sqrt(eta).

Partial trace contracts trace norm. Therefore

 ||Omega_RB-Omega'_RB||_1 <=2sqrt(eta)+eta.                 [3]

For Hermitian contractions A,B in A, centrality makes the reference-Choi
identity

 tau(B (Phi-E)(A))=Tr[(Omega_RB-Omega'_RB)(A^T tensor B)]

literal: Phi_phys(sigma A)=sigma Phi(A). Since ||A^T tensor B||<=1, [3]
plus trace duality proves [B] uniformly over the entire input unit ball.
No observation/measurement is chosen per promised state.

This proves a dimension-free restricted lifting statement outside the
supplied-instrument family: it works for ANY selfcompatible marginal whose
supplied basis has eta small, provided its broadcaster is supplied. It does
not assert that some such basis exists whenever m grows.

4. A DIRECT PINCHING VARIANT AND DIAGNOSTIC SCOPE

One can avoid storing the broadcaster in the OUTPUT formula at a larger
constant. Put E=E_D Phi. The Stinespring Schwarz defect of the broadcaster's
commuting receiver embeddings bounds

 ||[Phi(A),Phi(U_t)]||_2 <=2 sqrt(1-||Phi(U_t)||_2^2).

Consequently [1] and averaged Minkowski give

 ||Phi(A)-E_D Phi(A)||_2
   <=sqrt(2)(sqrt(l)+sqrt(v)) <=2sqrt(2 eta),

where l=average_t(1-||Phi(U_t)||_2^2), v=average_t||Phi(U_t)-U_t||_2^2,
and l+v=2eta exactly. This needs no positivity/selfadjointness. The final
formula is EB and reference preserving using only the marginal and D;
existence of the actual broadcaster is the mathematical hypothesis.

MAIN STATUS
The requested dimension-free CP-power-range EB lifting counterexample has
NOT been constructed. The main beta_m -> 0 gate remains UNRESOLVED.
There is instead a complete map-level obstruction to extending the supplied
Petz-corrected instrument representation to every admissible channel. Its
distance is constant in the actual normalized infinity-to-one norm, and
its counterexample has CP, UCP, faithful-reference fixation, L2 positivity,
selfadjointness and an explicit actual selfcompatible broadcaster.
Do not relabel this representation obstruction as an EB-lifting refutation.

STRONGEST EARNED CLAIM
For d >= 2, every canonical bistochastic instrument construction

 L(A)=sum_y K_y A K_y*, M_y=K_y* K_y,
 sum_y K_y* K_y=sum_y K_y K_y*=I,
 Psi(A)=sum_y Tr(M_y A)/Tr(M_y) M_y,
 C=(L+Psi)/2, Q=C* C

satisfies the Liouville-superoperator trace bound

 Tr_S Q <= d(d+3)/4.                                      (1)

Its closure under tensor products, convex mixtures, unitary conjugations,
and block pinching/direct sums obeys the same bound at the total matrix
dimension. Here * means the normalized tracial L2 adjoint.
The optimal universal 1->2 cloner marginal

 Phi_d(A)=lambda_d A+(1-lambda_d) Tr(A) I/d,
 lambda_d=(d+2)/(2(d+1))                                  (2)

has Tr_S Phi_d=d(d+1)/2 and is an actual positive selfadjoint bistochastic
selfcompatible channel. For d=2^q, q>=1, it is separated from the entire
corrected-instrument closure by

 inf_Q ||Phi_d-Q||_(infinity->1,tau)
       >= d/[4(d+1)] -> 1/4, tau=Tr/d.                    (3)

The input supremum here is over ALL Hermitian contractions, as in beta_m.
Thus absence of a representation theorem is a substantive map obstruction,
not just absence of a selected Kraus decomposition.
A second bound excludes even exact raw canonical append representations:

 Tr_S C <= (d^2+1)/2 < Tr_S Phi_d,                         (4)

although that separation shrinks in normalized infinity-to-one norm with d.

FULL DERIVATION OF THE CORRECTED TRACE OBSTRUCTION
Use an orthonormal basis of L2(M_d,tau). Every bistochastic UCP L contracts
this Hilbert space: Kadison Schwarz followed by trace preservation gives
||L(A)||_2^2 <= tau(L(A*A))=tau(A*A). Hence ||L||<=1 and
Tr_S L*L <= d^2.

Psi is the sum of positive rank-one Hilbert-space operators
 |M_y><M_y|_tau / tau(M_y). Therefore it is positive selfadjoint. Scalar
Cauchy-Schwarz gives Psi<=I. Its superoperator trace is exactly

 Tr_S Psi=sum_y tau(M_y^2)/tau(M_y)
          =sum_y Tr(M_y^2)/Tr(M_y)
          <=sum_y Tr(M_y)=d.                              (5)

The inequality uses sum_i mu_i^2 <= (sum_i mu_i)^2 separately for each
effect, not a rank or number-of-outcomes assumption.
Since 0<=Psi<=I, Tr_S Psi^2<=Tr_S Psi. Also Re L<=I because ||L||<=1,
so Re Tr_S(L*Psi)<=Tr_S Psi. Expanding C* C gives

 Tr_S C*C
   =(Tr_S L*L+Tr_S Psi^2+2 Re Tr_S L*Psi)/4
   <=(d^2+3d)/4.

This proves (1). No commutation of L and Psi is used. Nothing assumes
Psi<=Q. In particular this is consistent with the baseline exact SOS.

For tensors of dimensions a,b, trace multiplies and

 [a(a+3)/4][b(b+3)/4] <= ab(ab+3)/4,

because the difference after removing ab/16 is
4(ab+3)-(a+3)(b+3)=3(a-1)(b-1)>=0.
Induction treats every finite tensor product. Mixtures preserve the trace
bound by linearity, and unitary conjugation preserves supertrace. Pinched
block sums satisfy sum_j d_j(d_j+3)/4 <= d(d+3)/4 for d=sum_j d_j.
Thus these operations do not rescue exact corrected representation.

ACTUAL ADMISSIBLE COUNTEREXAMPLE MAP
Let F swap the two d-dimensional output factors and P+=(I+F)/2. Define on
EVERY input density, not just pure inputs,

 B_d(rho)=2/(d+1) P+(rho tensor I)P+.                     (6)

This is CP because tensoring with I and sandwiching by P+ are CP. It is TP:
Tr[P+(rho tensor I)]=[(d+1)/2]Tr rho. Equivalently Kraus matrices are
sqrt(2/(d+1)) P+(I tensor |k>), k=1,...,d; their adjoint products sum to I.
Both marginal maps coincide because output swap fixes P+ on its range.
Expanding P+ and using the elementary swap partial-trace identities gives

 Tr_2[P+(rho tensor I)P+]
    =[(d+2)rho+(Tr rho)I]/4.

Therefore each marginal is precisely (2). Thus Phi_d is selfcompatible
with a displayed ALL-INPUT broadcaster. It fixes the faithful reference
I/d, is UCP and TP, and on L2(M_d,tau) has eigenvalues 1 on I and lambda_d
on the orthogonal traceless subspace. These are positive, so L2 positivity
and selfadjointness are exact, without numerical SDP evidence.

Its supertrace is 1+(d^2-1)lambda_d=d(d+1)/2. Subtracting (1) yields the
supertrace gap d(d-1)/4.

For d=2^q take the d^2-1 nonidentity Hermitian Pauli strings P_j. These are
traceless contractions with P_j^2=I and are orthonormal in L2(tau). Both
Phi_d and Q fix I, so

 average_j tau[P_j (Phi_d-Q)(P_j)]
       =Tr_S(Phi_d-Q)/(d^2-1)
       >= d/[4(d+1)].

For each j, |tau(P_j X)|<=tau(|X|) when X=X*. At least one actual bounded
input P_j therefore attains the lower bound (3). This proves it in the
requested normalized infinity-to-one norm, rather than using Choi trace
or an entangled auxiliary input. The Pauli witness set is finite and exact.
No claim that its complete enumeration is efficient in q is necessary.

FULL DERIVATION OF THE RAW CANONICAL-INSTRUMENT OBSTRUCTION
For the same Kraus family, Tr_S L=sum_y |Tr K_y|^2. Polar decomposition gives
|Tr K_y|<=Tr sqrt(M_y). Set s=Tr M_y and p_i=mu_i/s for nonzero M_y.
For d>=2,

 (sum_i sqrt(p_i))^2+sum_i p_i^2 <= d+1/d.                (7)

Here is a proof rather than an import. Define
D=d-(sum_i sqrt(p_i))^2=sum_{i<j}(sqrt(p_i)-sqrt(p_j))^2.
Also sum_i p_i^2-1/d=(1/d)sum_{i<j}(p_i-p_j)^2.
Since (sqrt(p_i)+sqrt(p_j))^2<=2(p_i+p_j)<=2,

 sum_i p_i^2-1/d <= 2D/d.

Consequently the left side of (7) is at most
 d+1/d-(1-2/d)D<=d+1/d. Multiply by s, sum outcomes using sum_y s=d,
and combine with (5) to obtain Tr_S L+Tr_S Psi <= d^2+1. This proves (4).
The universal cloner has a strict gap (d-1)/2 above this raw trace cap.
This concerns the CANONICAL prepared state M_y/Tr M_y, not every arbitrary
outcome-dependent preparation or every possible instrument.

SECOND ATTACK: THE CLONER DOES NOT REFUTE EB POWER-RANGE LIFTING
For this actual counterexample to representation, powers are depolarizing:
Phi_d^m has parameter lambda_d^m. In fact their exact distance to all
trace-preserving EB maps in the normalized infinity-to-one norm is

 dist(Phi_d^m,EB_tau)=kappa_d max(0,lambda_d^m-1/(d+1)),
 kappa_d=4 floor(d^2/4)/d^2.                              (8)

To derive it, average any EB comparator over simultaneous unitary input
and output conjugation. Averaging preserves EB, TP, and cannot increase
this norm. A covariant TP map has depolarizing parameter eta. Its normalized
Choi state is eta |Omega><Omega|+(1-eta)I/d^2. A separable Choi state has
overlap with |Omega> at most 1/d, by the product-state Cauchy-Schwarz bound,
so eta<=1/(d+1). The endpoint is EB explicitly via the Haar measure-prepare
map d integral <psi|A|psi> |psi><psi| dpsi=(A+Tr(A)I)/(d+1). Convex mixing
with the depolarizer treats all 0<=eta<=1/(d+1). Its finite-dimensional
integral can be replaced by a finite convex decomposition; no efficient
outcome compiler is claimed.

The remaining norm is ||Id-Delta||_(infinity->1,tau)=kappa_d. The norm is
convex in the input contraction, so its maximum occurs at Hermitian
unitaries with k eigenvalues +1 and d-k eigenvalues -1. Direct calculation
gives 4k(d-k)/d^2, maximizing at floor(d/2) or ceil(d/2). This proves (8).
Since lambda_d<=2/3 for every d>=2,

 sup_d dist(Phi_d^m,EB_tau) <= (2/3)^m ->0.               (9)

Growing d beyond 2^m keeps these channels non-EB at every finite m, but
non-EB membership is NOT constant distance. The actual error in (8) still
vanishes. Thus neither cloner non-EB powers nor the structural obstruction
constitutes the assigned decisive beta_m counterexample.

FAILED ROUTES RETAINED
1. The supplied copy ladder fails the bounded likelihood sandwich and
capacity requirement; its known bounded-capacity pure variant classicalizes.
2. Antisymmetric/Werner extension Choi states can be far from separable in
ordinary trace norm while their induced maps have nontrivial L2 contraction
of order 1/d. Positive powers/tensors retain this small contraction; they
cannot provide constant infinity-to-one map error.
3. Sparse incompatible Pauli contexts randomized over flags give pairwise
small commutators with collective uncertainty. The baseline already excludes
this as an actual small-residual compatible channel by its collective SOS.
An abstract family is not the entire range of the required CP power map.
4. Fixed finite almost-commuting results do not justify a uniform bound over
the growing operator-norm unit ball; nonseparable ultraproduct lifting remains
a missing step, not a theorem or counterexample acquired here.
