C10_S01 INDEPENDENT INITIAL — ACQUIRED BOUNDARY FOSTER CERTIFICATE
Requested model/effort: gpt-6.1-sol/max. Observed backend model: UNKNOWN.
Origin: c10_s01, initial phase, no cycle6 peer reads or peer messages.
All claims below are self-derived candidates with complete written arguments.
No independent review, historical novelty or field-breaking result is claimed.

STRONGEST CLAIM AND EXACT ADMITTED INTERFACE

For every finite two-layer catalytic network of the following form, with every
displayed rate a fixed positive rational, a global rational norm-like Foster
function and explicit recovery core can be acquired by O(d+m) rational
arithmetic operations from the actual input rates. No supplied equilibrium,
Poisson stationary law, class membership oracle, deterministic absorber,
compact collar, real-field QE or unbounded coefficient enumeration is used.

There are nonempty root species R_i and disjoint child species Y_j. Each root
has reactions 0 -> R_i and R_i -> 0 with rates b_i and delta_i. Each child has
a nonempty set P_j of root parents; for each i in P_j there are reactions
  R_i -> R_i + Y_j,     R_i + Y_j -> R_i
with rates alpha_ij and beta_ij. There are no other reactions. Children never
act as catalysts. Root counts are denoted r_i and child counts y_j. This is
an actual binary stochastic mass-action model; its intensities are b_i,
delta_i r_i, alpha_ij r_i and beta_ij r_i y_j, respectively. Disabled
reactions have zero intensity at the integer boundary. No monomial/factorial
approximation is made. Every reaction has its reverse, hence the network is
weakly reversible and in particular endotactic.

The process is nonexplosive, irreducible on the entire lattice N^(r+s), and
positive recurrent. The certificate proves a physical-time drift at every
integer state, including all zero-catalyst faces. It gives an explicit
finite set K and E_x tau_K <= V(x) from every x outside K. It also gives
  E_x V(X_t) <= exp(-lambda t)V(x)
                 + (C/lambda)(1-exp(-lambda t)),
and its unique stationary law has E_pi V <= C/lambda. These bounds are for
this exactly recognized class. They do not settle arbitrary rational binary
weakly reversible recurrence or acquire a generic CRN Foster certificate.

CONSTRUCTED RATIONAL DATA

For each child j, set
  S_j = sum_{i in P_j} r_i,
  B_j = sum_{i in P_j} b_i,
  D_j = max_{i in P_j} delta_i,
  beta_j = min_{i in P_j} beta_ij,
  c_j = min(1, beta_j/D_j),
  gamma_j = min(c_j B_j/2, beta_j/2),
  g_j(S) = 1 + c_j/(S+1).
All c_j and gamma_j are positive. For each root choose
  w_i = [1 + sum_{j:i in P_j}(1+c_j)alpha_ij]/delta_i.
Define
  V(r,y) = sum_i w_i r_i + sum_j g_j(S_j)y_j,
  C = sum_i w_i b_i,
  lambda = min({1/w_i}_i union {gamma_j/(1+c_j)}_j),
  gamma_0 = min(1, min_j gamma_j).
If there are no children, omit the child sets and put gamma_0=1. Thus
  a_0 N <= V <= a_1 N,
where N=sum_i r_i+sum_j y_j, a_0=min({w_i}_i union {1})>0 and
a_1=max({w_i}_i union {1+c_j}_j). V is nonnegative, zero only at zero,
and norm-like. If a strictly positive Foster function is preferred use 1+V.

FULL DRIFT DERIVATION

Roots evolve autonomously: catalytic reactions leave each r_i unchanged.
Fix j and abbreviate S=S_j, c=c_j. A root immigration in P_j increases S
by one and changes g by
  -c/[(S+1)(S+2)].
For S>=1, each root death decreases S by one and changes g by
  c/[S(S+1)].
For S=0 there are no root deaths; the second term must be omitted. Therefore
  L_root g_j(S)
    = -c_j B_j/[(S+1)(S+2)]
        + 1_{S>=1} c_j [sum_{i in P_j}delta_i r_i]/[S(S+1)].
This identity is the decisive exact boundary correction. The expression
c_j D_j/(S+1) is an upper bound for the positive term only when S>=1;
it is not inserted at S=0.

Write the child's birth and per-particle death rates as
  h_j(r)=sum_i alpha_ij r_i,
  a_j(r)=sum_i beta_ij r_i.
Because changing a child does not change g_j, its contribution to LV is
  g_j h_j + y_j[L_root g_j - a_j g_j].
At S=0, h_j=a_j=0 and the bracket equals exactly -c_j B_j/2. At S>=1,
  L_root g_j - a_j g_j
    <= c_j D_j/(S+1) - beta_j S
    <= beta_j/(S+1) - beta_j S
    <= -beta_j/2.
The first inequality discards two nonpositive terms, the second uses
c_j D_j<=beta_j, and the last is valid for every integer S>=1.
Consequently, globally on the actual integer lattice,
  L_root g_j - a_j g_j <= -gamma_j.
Also g_j h_j <= (1+c_j)sum_i alpha_ij r_i. Summing all root and child
contributions gives
  LV <= sum_i w_i b_i
          + sum_i[-w_i delta_i
                   + sum_{j:i in P_j}(1+c_j)alpha_ij]r_i
          - sum_j gamma_j y_j
     = C - sum_i r_i - sum_j gamma_j y_j
     <= C - lambda V.                                      (F)
The final inequality uses lambda w_i<=1 and
lambda g_j<=lambda(1+c_j)<=gamma_j. There is no state sampling or asymptotic
drift remainder in this proof.

NONEXPLOSION AND IRREDUCIBILITY, WITHOUT A HIDDEN RECURRENCE PREMISE

The total molecule count N has positive changes only through root immigration
and catalytic births. Let b=sum_i b_i and A=max_i sum_j alpha_ij (A=0 if
there are no catalytic channels). Then
  LN <= b + A N.
All jumps change one count by one, and the total intensity is finite at
each state. Stopping at N>=M, Dynkin/Grönwall bounds the probability of
reaching M by a finite time by a quantity tending to zero as M->infinity.
For example use 1+N and LN<=max(b,A)(1+N), giving
  P_x(tau_M<=t) <= exp(max(b,A)t)(1+N(x))/(1+M).
Explosion within any finite set is impossible because its rates are bounded.
This proves nonexplosion in physical time independently of (F). It is a
special case of the familiar linear nonexplosion criterion, rather than a
new general regularity theorem.

For any two lattice states, first make every root positive using its direct
immigration. Adjust every child to its requested destination count using
one chosen parent, which stays positive. Then adjust each root to its
requested destination count. Every step has positive finite intensity, and
there is a finite positive-probability path following the selected reactions.
Hence the entire lattice is one irreducible communicating class. In
particular, no unidentified nonlinear component invariant is being erased.

EFFECTIVE RECOVERY AND STATIONARY CONSEQUENCES

Let R=max(1,ceil[(C+1)/gamma_0]) and K={x:N(x)<R}. From (F), outside K,
  LV <= C-gamma_0 N <= -1.
For tau_K stopped also on a finite box, Dynkin gives
  E_x(tau_K wedge t wedge tau_box) <= V(x).
Use nonexplosion, Fatou and then monotone convergence to obtain
E_x tau_K<=V(x). The standard Foster criterion for an irreducible countable
CTMC gives positive recurrence. Alternatively the following moment bound
provides tight Cesàro laws and thus an invariant probability, which with
irreducibility/nonexplosion implies positive recurrence.

Apply stopped Dynkin to exp(lambda t)V(X_t). Its drift is at most
C exp(lambda t) by (F). Fatou upon removing the finite-box stop gives the
displayed moment bound at every finite t. Cesàro laws are tight because
V>=a_0 N. On a countable state space, take a convergent subsequence; for
each fixed s, the Cesàro measure differs from its image under P_s by total
variation at most 2s/T. This proves invariance of the limit (bounded test
functions can be approximated uniformly on the common tight core). Fatou
then gives E_pi V<=C/lambda.

There is a computable exponential return-time variant. Put W=1+V and
choose a finite core K_e outside which
W>=2(C+lambda)/lambda. Then
  LW<=-(lambda/2)W outside K_e.
Stopped Dynkin for exp[(lambda/2)(t wedge tau_Ke)]W gives
  E_x exp[(lambda/2)tau_Ke] <= W(x) for x outside K_e.
One can take K_e to be a molecule-count threshold since W>=1+a_0 N.
This is an exponential moment of a physical-time return, not of the stationary
count vector and not an assertion that exp(theta V) is itself a Lyapunov
function. Minorization/mixing constants have not been acquired in this
INITIAL snapshot.

NON-COMPLEX-BALANCED EXAMPLE AND EXECUTED EVIDENCE

Take roots A,C with both direct immigration and death rates 1, and child B:
  A <-> A+B with forward/reverse rates 1,1;
  C <-> B+C with forward/reverse rates 2,1.
At the leaf complex A+B, complex balance would require c_B=1. At the leaf
B+C it would require c_B=2. Thus no positive complex-balanced equilibrium
exists. This is not merely the familiar Poisson product-form subclass.
The reaction graph has 5 complexes, one linkage, rank 3, deficiency 1.
The constructed certificate is c_B=1, gamma_B=1/2,
  w_A=3, w_C=5, C=8, lambda=1/5,
  V=3A+5C+[1+1/(A+C+1)]B.
At A=C=0, LV=8-B, so the boundary is recovered with actual negative drift.
The formula's conservative chosen return core is N<18.

Executable exact arithmetic:
  work/cycle6/c10_s01/two_layer_certificate.py
  work/cycle6/c10_s01/certificate_checks.json
The program acquires the rational certificate for three input fixtures,
constructs raw integer source/target vectors, evaluates the actual falling-
factorial generator, and compares it with (F). It checked 1,548 states with
integer coordinates 0..5, including 48 all-catalyst-zero states. All checks
passed; final local elapsed time about 0.21s. It also rejected a downstream
catalyst outside the promised two-layer syntax. These finite diagnostics
test implementation and boundary handling; the all-state claim rests on the
complete inequalities above, not the finite checks. No generic solver,
infinite-state trajectory, proof assistant or external validation was run.

RETAINED FAILED SHORTCUTS

1. Any positive linear function of molecule counts fails to be a strict
Foster function on the zero-root face with arbitrary children: child events
are disabled, while root immigration gives a strictly positive constant
drift. The reciprocal term is load-bearing, not cosmetic.

2. Exponentiating the constructed V is false in general. For the simple
network 0<->A, A<->A+B with every rate 1, the construction gives
V=3A+[1+1/(A+1)]B. At (A,B)=(1,n), the four jump increments are
-3+n/2, 3-n/6, 3/2, -3/2, with intensities 1,1,1,n.
For every theta>0 the first exponential grows in n faster than the negative
term n(exp(-3theta/2)-1), so L exp(theta V)/exp(theta V)>0 eventually.
At n=60 and theta=2 log 2, the ratio is the exact positive rational
295147905179352047617/16384. The script checks this witness exactly. An
exponential-return estimate from LW<=-eta W is valid; a naive stationary-
exponential-count estimate from exp(theta V) is not.

3. Children acting as catalysts fall outside this synthesis: root autonomy
used in L_root g_j fails. Arbitrary extra reactions can also destroy (F).
No extension to a catalytic feedback cycle is inferred from a finite test.

4. The N76 residual-factorial inequality is not assumed to imply negative
drift, and its reaction-event upward bound is not used as a physical-time
nonexplosion theorem. The reciprocal corrector closes that drift gap only
for the explicit class above. N77's classwise escaping laws remain intact;
our entire lattice is actually proved irreducible, so its tightness is a
component conclusion, not a transfer across unidentified components.

coefficients and count thresholds are acquired rational numbers. Computing
them uses O(d+m) rational operations and polynomial bit work in the explicit
input length; denominators of finite sums/products have total encoded height
bounded polynomially by that length. The finite core is represented by a
count threshold; enumerating all of its lattice points is not required and
can be exponentially costly. Evaluating V and its raw generator uses only
rational arithmetic; count encoding and denominators S_j+1 are charged.

The recovery bound is in the rate model's physical time units. It does not
turn a possibly very large numerical C/lambda or R into a polynomial-time
simulation. Quadratic catalytic firing intensity can be much larger than
the boundary recovery rate. The linear drift certificate alone does not
bound expected total reaction firings up to an unbounded return time; that
cost is UNKNOWN here. No thermodynamic energy or hardware cost is modeled.
Peer costs and backend compute/tokens are UNKNOWN. No financial spending,
large computation, source redistribution, external message or commit occurred.

C10_S02 INDEPENDENT INITIAL — 7 OCTOBER 2026
Rational bimolecular obstruction to two proposed stochastic certificate transfers
Worker c10_s02; requested GPT-6.1 Sol / max; observed model UNKNOWN.

Status: exact internal counterexamples; no field-breaking or priority claim.

STRONGEST CLAIM

A three-species, six-reaction, unit-rate, reversible deficiency-zero bimolecular
network has a proved single rational deterministic absorber and a nonexplosive
positive-recurrent stochastic component containing strictly positive states, but:
(i) the N76 residual-factorial potential F has L F(a,0,1)=log a>0 for every a>=2;
    every strictly increasing scalar transform of F fails strict Foster drift;
(ii) every globally coercive concave potential, including every finite concave
    affine minimum of arbitrary size/bit height, fails strict Foster drift;
(iii) from positive count states, coordinate-zero states are visited almost
    surely; stationary P(A=0)=1/(e+1) at volume one.

These falsify specified certificate/containment transfers, NOT componentwise
positive recurrence, stationary tightness, thermodynamic concentration,
nonconcave corrections, or general bimolecular endotactic recurrence. This
example actually has a light-tailed invariant law. N77's higher-degree escaping
family is a separate quantifier obstruction, not replicated here.

1. EXACT NETWORK

Species A,B,C; unit volume; all six rational rate constants one:
    A+C <-> A+B <-> B,    C <-> 0.
Reaction order and stochastic propensities:
    A+C -> A+B: ac;   A+B -> A+C: ab;
    A+B -> B: ab;     B -> A+B: b;
    C -> 0: c;       0 -> C: 1.
All sources AND products have molecularity at most two; factorial mass action
is exact here because sources have no repeated species. Complexes are
0,C,B,A+B,A+C. Two linkage classes, stoichiometric rank three, deficiency
5-2-3=0. Reversibility implies weak reversibility and endotacticity: an outward
edge from an essential w-maximal source would give its reverse an essential
source at strictly larger w-height, a contradiction.
Vectors (0,1,-1),(-1,0,0),(0,0,1) span R^3. There is one positive deterministic
class R^3_{>0}, and the count component below is genuinely infinite.

2. COMPLETE DETERMINISTIC ABSORBER PROOF

The ODE is
    a'=b(1-a),  b'=a(c-b),  c'=a(b-c)+1-c.                 (D)
For a positive initial state, let l=min(a(0),1)>0, U=max(a(0),1).
Exactly a(t)-1=(a(0)-1)exp(-integral_0^t b), so l<=a(t)<=U.
Choose U/(U+1)<q<1 and H=b+qc. Then
    H'=-(1-q)ab+[(1-q)a-q]c+q
       <=-d_b b-d_c c+q,
with d_b=(1-q)l>0 and d_c=q-(1-q)U>0. Taking
delta=min(d_b,d_c/q)>0 gives H'<=-delta H+q. Thus b,c are bounded
and the positive solution is global. Since c'>=1-(U+1)c,
liminf c>=1/(U+1). Since b'>=l c-U b, b has an eventual positive
lower bound. Therefore integral b diverges and a->1 exponentially eventually.
For z=(b,c), z'=M(a)z+(0,1) with
    M(a)=[[-a,a],[a,-a-1]].
For e=z-(1,1),
    e'=M(1)e+(a-1)[[-1,1],[1,-1]]z.
M(1) has eigenvalues (-3+sqrt5)/2 and (-3-sqrt5)/2, both negative.
The forcing tends to zero because z is bounded; variation of constants implies
e->0. Every positive initial condition therefore converges to (1,1,1), without
importing a general global-attractor theorem or N33's candidate proof.

Q=[1/2,2]^3 is invariant. On A lower/upper facets a'=b/2>0 and -b<0.
On B lower/upper facets a(c-b)>=0 and <=0. On C lower facet
c'=a(b-1/2)+1/2>=1/2; on C upper facet c'=a(b-2)-1<=-1.
Convergence implies eventual entry into this same rational cube from EVERY
positive deterministic start. Entry time can depend on the unbounded start.

3. EXACT STOCHASTIC COMPONENTS AND RECURRENCE

Two closed communicating components partition all counts:
    Gamma_0={(0,0,c):c>=0},
    Gamma={(a,b,c) in N_0^3:a+b>=1}.
The predicate a+b=0 is preserved by every enabled reaction. On Gamma_0 there
is only C immigration/death. Every Gamma state communicates with (1,0,0):
if a=0,b>=1 use B->A+B; with a>=1 create C, convert C to B using A+C->A+B,
and consume one A using A+B->B, repeating until a=1. Then convert all B to C
using A+B->A+C and remove C. Reverse the finite path using reversed reactions.
All paths have positive rates. Gamma contains (1,1,1), every (a,0,1), and
every (0,n,0): the obstructions are inside ONE positive-containing component.

For V_0=a+b+c,
    L V_0=b-ab+1-c<=V_0+1.
Stopped Dynkin/Gronwall at first count-ball exit gives
P(sup_{s<=t} V_0(X_s)>=N)<=(V_0(X_0)+1)e^t/(N+1).
Jumps change V_0 by at most one; finite balls have bounded total rates.
Explosion would require exits from all count balls, so N->infinity proves
nonexplosion. This is an explicit derivation, not an imported regularity claim.

On Gamma the normalized invariant law is
    pi(a,b,c)=1/[(e^3-e)a!b!c!].                          (P)
Its normalizer is e^3-e. Each reverse edge satisfies detailed balance by
    (1/x!)x_under_y=1/(x-y)!
                  =(1/(x-y+y')!)(x-y+y')_under_y'.
The finite invariant probability, irreducibility and nonexplosion imply positive
recurrence by the standard countable-CTMC theorem (also Xu Corollary 4.17).
Gamma_0 has Poisson(1) C. Both stationary laws are tight/light-tailed.
On Gamma, pi(A=0)=e^-1(1-e^-1)/(1-e^-2)=1/(e+1)>0. Recurrence implies
that from (1,1,1), (0,1,0) is visited almost surely and repeatedly. Thus
deterministic permanence supplies neither infinite pathwise positive containment
nor arbitrarily high stationary mass in a strict-positive core at fixed volume.

With standard volume-V intensities ac/V,ab/V,ab/V,b,c,V, invariant counts are
independent Poisson(V) conditioned on a+b>=1, and pi_V(A=0)=1/(e^V+1).
Each finite V still has almost-sure boundary visits; the stationary boundary
mass is exponentially small as V grows. This does not contradict finite-horizon
N35 or high-volume concentration, and does not give an escaping component family.

4. EXACT N76 F COUNTEREXAMPLE, INCLUDING ALL MONOTONE TRANSFORMS

Use F(x)=min_available_y log((x-y)!), vector factorial, for the complex set
{0,C,B,A+B,A+C}. At x_a=(a,0,1), a>=2, exp F=(a-1)!.
Only the following jumps are enabled:
    A+C->A+B, rate a, target (a,1,0), exp F ratio 1;
    C->0, rate 1, target (a,0,0), exp F ratio a;
    0->C, rate 1, target (a,0,2), exp F ratio 1.
These ratios follow from the exact available residual minima: respectively
(a-1)!, a!, and (a-1)!. Therefore, for every a>=2 and theta>0,
    L F(x_a)=log a>0,
    L exp(theta F)(x_a)/exp(theta F(x_a))=a^theta-1>0.
For ANY strictly increasing h for which its generator is defined,
    L[h(F)](x_a)=h(log a!)-h(log (a-1)!)>0.                (F)
Thus every increasing scalar reparametrization fails a strict negative Foster
condition outside a finite set on Gamma. At z_n=(0,n,0), n>=1, both enabled
jumps preserve F, so L F(z_n)=0 as well.
The N76 individual-reaction inequality is preserved; an upward event-chain
bound does not imply negative continuous-time Foster drift.

5. NO COERCIVE CONCAVE FOSTER POTENTIAL

Let W be concave on D={a,b,c>=0:a+b>=1}, and coercive on Gamma, meaning
W(x)->infinity along every Gamma integer sequence with norm->infinity.
Along any coordinate ray staying in D, this forces strict increase across
every unit step: if a concave one-variable g has g(t+1)<=g(t), later integer
secants are no larger, and g remains bounded above, contradicting coercivity.
At z_n=(0,n,0), the only jumps are
    B->A+B, rate n, displacement +e_A;
    0->C, rate 1, displacement +e_C.
Both strictly increase W, so
    L W(z_n)=n[W(z_n+e_A)-W(z_n)]
              +[W(z_n+e_C)-W(z_n)]>0                    (C)
for every n>=1. Hence no such W satisfies L W<=-epsilon off a finite set,
nor a geometric Foster inequality L W<=-epsilon W+b 1_K.

For W=min_{j<=m}(alpha_j dot x+beta_j), a sharper elementary proof applies.
Gamma contains coordinate rays (a,0,0), (0,b,0), (1,0,c); each affine form
upper-bounds W, so every alpha_{j,A},alpha_{j,B},alpha_{j,C} must be positive
for W to be coercive. Let alpha_A=min_j alpha_{j,A}, alpha_C=min_j alpha_{j,C}.
Then L W(z_n)>=n alpha_A+alpha_C>0. This holds for arbitrary real/rational
coefficients and any finite facet count. More facets/bit precision/search cannot
repair this architecture. Nonconcave, phase-corrected, skeleton or path methods
are not excluded. N33/N50 retain their separate DETERMINISTIC scope; this is a
no-go only for a proposed direct stochastic transfer or named concave architecture.

C10_S03 INDEPENDENT INITIAL — EFFECTIVE RATIONAL BOUNDARY RECOVERY
7 October 2026. Author c10_s03; requested GPT-6.1 Sol/max, observed UNKNOWN.

STRONGEST CLAIM AND STATUS
A complete candidate derivation and dependency-free exact implementation give
an acquired rational Foster/boundary-recovery certificate for the precisely
admitted open bimolecular class below. It has a finite total bit/runtime bound,
not an unbounded enumeration promise. The certificate is valid at EVERY count
state, including disabled-reactant boundary states, uniformly over predictable
history-dependent controls in a supplied positive rational rate box. It proves
nonexplosion and a computable finite expected hitting time to counts at least q
of every species. Fixed time-homogeneous rates have one positive recurrent
closed communicating class containing zero. These are internal derivations,
not independently reviewed or formally machine-proved results.

This does not recognize all bimolecular endotactic networks. A detailed-balanced
bimolecular counterexample below is positively recurrent yet is rejected by the
compiler. LP/Foster tests and support-exhaustion constructions have substantial
published prior art. The present concrete contribution is their exact rational,
fully costed, robust boundary-path implementation, including a hybrid diagonal
quadratic admission test. Novelty of this packaging/bound is UNKNOWN. A general
endotactic stochastic recovery theorem and field-breaking advance remain OPEN.

INPUT AND ADMITTED CLASS
Input consists of d>=1 species, R>=1 integer reactions y_r -> y'_r with
|y_r|_1,|y'_r|_1 <=2, supplied rational bounds 0<l_r<=u_r, and integer q>=1.
The propensity is exactly k_r(t,past) (x)_under_y_r, with
(x)_under_y=product_i x_i!/(x_i-y_i)! if x>=y, otherwise zero.
Coefficients k_r may be any predictable processes taking values in their boxes.
There is no point-estimate, real-number oracle, stationary-distribution oracle,
positive-orthant surrogate, fitted volume limit, or supplied Lyapunov weight.
Volume factors, if used, must first be included in the rational rate bounds.

The compiler checks three structural conditions and one Foster condition:
(A) The complex reaction graph is weakly reversible, checked by reachability.
This places the class inside endotactic networks. Weak reversibility is not
used by the stochastic proof once the following acquired certificates exist.
(B) In the graph of unary reactions e_i -> e_j or e_i ->0, every species has a
directed path to0. Acquire a simple path of length <=d for each species.
(C) Starting with no produced species, repeatedly add a species occurring in
the product of a reaction all of whose reactant species were already produced.
The resulting support closure must contain every species. Acquire one such
reaction per species, ordered acyclically by its production dependencies.
(D) For some partition S,T of species and w_i>=1, write
 c_y(w,k)=sum_{r:y_r=y} k_r w.(y'_r-y_r).
For all box rates, require:
  c_e_i <=-1 for i in T;
  c_2e_i <=-1 for i in S, and c_2e_i <=0 for i in T;
  c_(e_i+e_j) <=0 for i!=j.
No restriction is imposed on first-order source coefficients for i in S.
If a required source is absent, its coefficient is0; a required <=-1 test then
fails. This is an explicitly defined sufficient class, not a claim that all
endotactic systems satisfy (B)-(D). Strict negative margins of an unnormalized
positive weight can be scaled to these normalized inequalities.

EXACT SYNTHESIS, SOUNDNESS, AND TERMINATION IN THIS CLASS
For each of the 2^d partitions S,T, each source-wise rate-box vertex gives a
rational linear inequality in w. Affinity in the independent rates makes these
vertex inequalities equivalent to the full-box conditions. Add w_i>=1. The
resulting rational polyhedron lies in the positive orthant and has no line.
If it is nonempty it has a vertex: minimize sum_i w_i below any feasible sum;
the resulting nonempty minimizing face is compact, and a vertex of this face
is a vertex of the original polyhedron. At a vertex d active constraints have
independent normals. Enumerate every d-row basis, solve it exactly, and check
all inequalities. Thus synthesis terminates and succeeds exactly when (D)
holds. A failed search rejects this certificate class only.

The implementation uses fractions.Fraction and exact Gaussian elimination.
It first checks the all-one weight as a verified candidate, then exhausts bases
if needed. It neither invokes a floating LP solver nor assumes efficient CAD.
The acyclic support grammar and unary drain paths are acquired from the input.

GLOBAL FOSTER INEQUALITY — EXACT AT THE BOUNDARY
Set W(x)=w.x, N(x)=sum_i x_i and wmax=max_i w_i. For any acquired w,S,T, compute
 b0=sum_{r:y_r=0} u_r w.y'_r >=0,
 c_i=max_box c_e_i(w,k) for i in S,
 g_i=max(0,c_i+2),
 B=b0+(1/4)sum_{i in S}g_i^2.
All these quantities are exact rationals. The generator satisfies
                L_t W(x) <= B-N(x) <= B-W(x)/wmax.             (F)
Indeed, source e_i contributes at most -x_i for i in T. Sources 2e_i for i in T
and every mixed quadratic source contribute nonpositively because their exact
falling factorials are nonnegative. For i in S, the first/diagonal pair is at
most c_i x_i-x_i(x_i-1) =-x_i^2+(c_i+1)x_i. For every x_i>=0,
 -x_i^2+(c_i+1)x_i <= -x_i + g_i^2/4,
by completing the square in -x_i^2+(c_i+2)x_i; if c_i+2<0 its maximum on the
nonnegative half-line is0. Summing proves (F), including x_i=0 or1. No molecular
Taylor error or deterministic concentration assumption enters this identity.

Nonexplosion follows directly rather than from deterministic permanence. Stop
at first W>=m. Since L W<=B and W>=0, the stopped Dynkin bound gives
 P(reach W>=m before t) <=(W(x)+Bt)/m.
A bounded W region contains finitely many count states, each with a uniformly
bounded total intensity, so infinitely many jumps there in finite time are
impossible. Letting m increase establishes nonexplosion for all admitted
predictable controls. The same argument gives E W(X_t)<=W(x)+Bt.

Define Rw=2 wmax(B+1), K={W<=Rw}, and Rn=ceil(Rw). Outside K, (F) is at most -1.
Stopping Dynkin at K, large W and time t gives E(tau_K stopped)<=W(x), since
its terminal W is nonnegative. Nonexplosion and Fatou yield
                         E_x tau_K <= W(x).                  (R)
The argument is conditional on every current history, hence may be restarted
under non-Markov controls.

BOUNDED GLOBAL BOUNDARY RECOVERY — NO SUPPLIED PATH OR BASIN
Every molecule in a state x in K can be sent along its acquired unary drain
path. Suppress competing firings for this witness path. The total count never
increases, and at most d N(x)<=d Rn reactions suffice to reach zero. Molecules
need not be physically labeled: a desired unary reaction is enabled whenever
at least one molecule of its source remains.

For a newly acquired species i, define Produce(i) recursively: manufacture one
extra molecule for every occurrence in the selected reaction's reactant, using
recipes of earlier species, then fire that reaction. Inductively, this word is
enabled from EVERY initial x and ends at a state >=x+e_i. The extra reactants
cancel the consumed counts and the selected product contains the new species.
Source size<=2 gives L_i<=1+2 max(previous L), hence L_i<=2^i-1 in acquisition
order. Repeating Produce(i) q times for every species gives an acquired word
of length A<=q(2^(d+1)-d-2), ending at a state >=q in every coordinate.
The script uses the actual word length A when it is smaller.

Let G_q={x_i>=q for all i}. For q=2 every admitted complex is enabled in G_q.
Combine the drain and production witnesses, and define exact rational data
 H=d Rn+A, D=max(Rn,2A), U=sum_r u_r, Q=U(D+1)^2,
 lmin=min_r l_r, alpha=lmin/(2Q), p=alpha^H, t0=H/Q.
Every state along the witness has N<=D. Each enabled desired firing has rate
at least lmin and total competing rate at most Q, because 0th/1st/2nd-order
factorials are <=(D+1)^2. The probability that the next event is the desired
reaction and occurs within1/Q is at least
 lmin integral_0^(1/Q) exp(-Qs) ds >= lmin/(2Q)=alpha.
This bound is valid after conditioning on every past history; upper and lower
hazard bounds persist under arbitrary admissible predictable controls.
The witness has <=H steps, so from every state in K,
                         P(tau_G_q<=t0)>=p.                  (P)
This is a bound on an actual molecular path, not just graph connectivity.

Attempt recovery for t0 time units from K. On a failed attempt, wait to return
to K and retry. Its unconditional expected return cost is at most the expected
W at the end of the attempt, at most Rw+B t0 by the stopped drift bound. Thus
every attempted cycle has expected cost at most Rw+(B+1)t0. Conditional failure
probability is <=1-p, so summing the geometric tail and using (R) proves
       E_x tau_G_q <= W(x) + [Rw+(B+1)t0]/p.                  (HIT)
Every number in the displayed bound is acquired and rational. The potentially
very small p is retained explicitly; this is not a polynomial physical-time
claim or an uncharged rare-event shortcut.

For fixed time-homogeneous rates, the same attempt argument using only the
drain word proves a finite expected hitting time to0 from every count state.
A return to0 after departure has finite expected duration: its initial holding
time is finite since support production requires a positive zero-source rate,
and any first jump has W<=2wmax. All states reachable from0 communicate back
to0 by the drain word and form a closed class Gamma. No different closed class
can exist because every count state has a path to0. Finite mean return to0
therefore gives positive recurrence of Gamma and a unique stationary law there.
This conclusion does not assign invariant distributions to history-dependent
controllers.

TOTAL BIT/RUNTIME BOUND AND CHARGED COSTS
Let b bound numerator/denominator bit lengths of every input rate endpoint,
and let L be the ordinary dense binary input length including q. Integer
complex entries lie in {0,1,2}. After clearing source-wise denominators, every
inequality has integer coefficient/RHS height at most 2^h, where a sufficient
 h=R b+ceil(log2(2R+1))+1.
There are at most C=3d+R 2^R inequalities per partition. Hadamard/Cramer's rule
bounds every numerator/denominator of a feasible vertex weight by 2^t for
 t=d h+ceil((d/2)log2(max(d,1)))+2.
Exact basis enumeration costs at most 2^d binom(C,d) times a polynomial in
C,d,h,t in ordinary bit operations. This is a finite worst-case algorithm,
not the unimplemented general polynomial LP claim from a numerical solver.

The compact weight, B, Rw,Rn,H,D,Q,alpha, grammar and drain graph have polynomial
binary description size in L. Actual expanded production words have length
at most q 2^(d+1), so expansion is already potentially exponential in binary q.
The numeric core radius Rn is at most 2^poly(L), and H is too. Expanding the
rational p=alpha^H and the recovery constant requires at most 2^poly(L) output
bits; exponentiation and exact arithmetic can be performed within that same
singly exponential type of bound. A conservative combined bound for all
synthesis, word expansion, rational output and checking is 2^O(L^3) bit
operations and bits. No general claim of polynomial total runtime is made.
A compact certificate may retain (alpha,H) as a rational-power expression;
its exact expanded value is computable within the stated bound.

Physical use has a different cost: the finite expected recovery bound can be
astronomically large. The algorithm does not execute that recovery, simulate
all firings, establish small stationary boundary mass, or price fuel/control
energy. Evaluation of actual propensities, volume factors, count encoding and
reaction firings are charged separately. Tokens, backend compute, energy and
end-to-end research CPU are UNKNOWN; no paid job or download was used.

EXACT IMPLEMENTATION / EXECUTED EVIDENCE
Frozen files: initial/compiler.py and initial/checks.json in this worker's
work directory. Run python3 initial/compiler.py within this directory to replay.
The final independent run measured 0.17569 seconds by perf_counter on the
shared host; this is just this small diagnostic process, not total research
compute, concurrency certification or a large-network scalability benchmark.

1. Nonlinear assembly, all unlisted boxes [1,2]:
  0->A; A->0 in[8,10]; A->B; B->A; A->A+B; A+B->C; C->A.
Its complex graph is strongly connected. The exact compiler acquires
 w=(1,9/4,13/4), S empty, B=2, Rw=39/2,Rn=20.
No primitive birth/death reaction for B or C is supplied. Their drain paths
are B->A->0 and C->A->0; C is produced using a genuinely binary reaction.
Actual q=2 production length A=14, H=74,D=28,Q=18502,
 alpha=1/37004, t0=37/9251, p=(1/37004)^74.
The expanded rational recovery constant has1128-bit height. 553 exact bases
were solved before finding this feasible weight. No optimal-weight claim.

2. Nonlinear autocatalysis: 0->A rate1, A->0 rate1, A->2A rate2,
2A->A rate1. The first-order-only compiler correctly rejects: its source A
coefficient is positive for every positive w. The hybrid compiler acquires
w=1,S={A}, B=13/4,Rw=17/2,Rn=9,H=11,Q=500,alpha=1/1000.
This is the deeper extension over the initial linear-only route, not a finite
observation promoted to a general theorem. The exact polynomial drift is
1+2n-n^2, and square completion proves its global certificate.

Executed exact diagnostics total4044 successful checks: 525 complete-generator
state checks,3459 independently enumerated rate-vertex/state checks,30 unary
drain word simulations and30 production-dominance simulations. Checks include
zero/one-molecule states. They diagnose the implementation; the uniform proof
is the symbolic derivation above. No stochastic trajectory was simulated.

PRECISE REJECTION / FRONTIER OBSTRUCTION
All rates1 in 0<->A,0<->B,A<->A+B,B<->A+B give a strongly connected bimolecular
weakly reversible graph. Every reversible edge is detailed-balanced by means
(1,1): for pi(a,b)=e^-2/(a!b!), direct neighboring-state balance holds for
immigration/death and for the birth-B rate a versus reverse rate a(b+1), and
symmetrically for A. The normalized product-Poisson law and the published
nonexplosion theorem imply positive recurrence. Equivalently its elementary
reversible stationary construction already shows the ordinary stable case.
Yet no compiler certificate exists: no diagonal 2A or2B source permits S to
be nonempty, and for S empty the first-source inequalities require
 -w_A+w_B<=-1 and w_A-w_B<=-1, a contradiction. Its total-count drift is
2-2ab and remains positive along the zero-species axes. This is the missing
multistep boundary mechanism. Failure of our certificate is not recurrence
failure, and the compiler does not report it as such.

PRIMARY SOURCES / PRIORITY AND IMPORT BOUNDARIES
- Gupta, Briat, Khammash (2014), PLoS Computational Biology1003669,
  https://journals.plos.org/ploscompbiol/article?id=10.1371/journal.pcbi.1003669
  Published linear Foster/LP conditions and nonlinear quadratic-drift tests
  already cover important bimolecular subclasses. The central Lyapunov/LP
  idea here is established prior art; precise compiler/bit-bound priority was
  not settled. Read article sections on bimolecular networks, Props10/11.
- Gupta, Khammash (2013/2014), https://arxiv.org/html/1312.2879
  Lemma3.1 already supplies support exhaustion and accessible positive states.
  Our support grammar is an explicit bounded reconstruction of that mechanism,
  not a claimed new reachability principle. The unary drain restriction and
  rational hazard/retry bound give the deliberately simpler acquisition scope.
- Briat, Khammash (2017), https://arxiv.org/html/1703.00319v3
  Robust rate uncertainty and algebraic ergodicity tests are already studied;
  Theorem3 restates conserved-bimolecular/negative-linear-drift prior art.
- Xu (2026 revision), https://arxiv.org/html/2409.05340v2
  Second-order endotactic regularity is reported; the general bimolecular
  weakly reversible positive-recurrence question remains open in its discussion.
  It does not give the boundary recovery compiler above. Weak reversibility's
  implication of endotacticity is established, not newly claimed here.
- Anderson et al., https://arxiv.org/html/1808.05328v2
  Preserve the published strongly-endotactic explosive higher-order boundary
  counterexample. No deterministic-permanence transfer was used in this proof.
Astra N34/N35/N50 and N76 were read at pinned revision37a01798e92a0763e64a4649ae00024079d2a266;
N76's full code/proof packet is absent and its event-chain upward estimate was
not imported as a negative Foster inequality. No candidate Astra theorem is a
load-bearing premise of this reconstruction.

UNRESOLVED GATES / NEXT DECISIVE ATTACK
Independent detailed review of (HIT), rate-control hazard conditioning, height
bounds and implementation is still required. Source tests are not that review.
No proof of new theorem-level priority, polynomial total cost, small stationary
boundary mass or general bimolecular recognition is supplied. The strongest
next route is a finite-step/skeleton Foster certificate that accounts for
cross-interaction recovery along a large boundary axis; the detailed-balanced
rejection above isolates why a one-step affine certificate can miss it.
Continuation will go to revisions/ and FINAL.txt. INITIAL.txt is immutable.

Memory was used only for review discipline (MEMORY.md84-87,91); no unverified
memory-derived fact supplied a mathematical assumption. Current baseline and
primary sources were read in this run.
